# 独り言・待機中・雑談のセリフ

[セリフ集の一覧](README.md)

全セリフ集から、見出しが Idle・Ambient・Chatter・Banter・Citizens などの節（場面を問わず口にするセリフ）と、街の住民の雑談・ラジオのページ（Cetus・Fortuna・Duviri の住民・オービターのラジオ）を集めたもの。英語原文。[WARFRAME Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/) の記事を元にしたもの（[CC BY-NC-SA 3.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc-sa/3.0/deed.ja)）。セリフの権利は Digital Extremes Ltd. にある。

### Amir/Quotes

[全セリフ](amir.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Amir/Quotes)

**Amir's Shockwave › Radio Chatter**

- Amir: [sound of things being knocked over] "Sorry! Sorry. I... I saw a spider." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0430Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Höllvania Bowl. Where the ball never stops rolling... Until we get hit by a technozombie apocalypse. Ball stopped rolling pretty quick after that. Yeeeeah." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0440Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Come on down to Twisty Top, where we have 8,762 flavors of soft serve, like "Techrot", "Grass", and "Oh Sol, Why Is It Savory?"" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0450Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You're now listening to... 102.3 Real Rock FM Where we play nothin' but rock... ...rock... ....and more ROCK!!! This ain't your granny's station!" (subtitle appears: Now Playing - The Great Despair by On-lyne) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0570Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Sometimes I think about how many of you are listening to me. And then I panic and wee myself a little." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0580Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You ever just sit in your Orbiter and think about stuff? No? Cool cool cool cool cool cool cool cool." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0640Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "If there's a long pause, it's not for dramatic effect. I just tripped over the cord again." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0690Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Coming to you from a Capitalist Hellscape in a room with no windows! By choice! Mostly!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0700Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "If you're sitting in the dark alone at 3 A.M. — first of all, SAMESIES. Second of all, drink some water. Seriously. Hydrate." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0710Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "If you're looking for the good shrooms, you gotta go underground. Trust me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0720Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Word on the street - well under the street - is that there's a futuristic city sitting on top of something REAL interesting. Caps off to whoever finds it first. Get it? Caps? Like mushroom - okay moving on." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0730Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Say "Roots & Shoots" one more time, I dare you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0740Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You know what they say: When one door closes, a weiiiiird tunnel to a mushroom trip opens up underneath a futuristic city. Nobody says that? Huh." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0750Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "My mom used to say my laundry would grow mushrooms if I didn't air it out. I wonder if that's what happened in the Deepmines..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0760Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "... What the Hell is a Sprodling?! WAIT... WAIT I WANT ONE. DRIFTER - BRING ME BACK A SPRODLING RIGHT NOW. RIGHT. NOW." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0770Amir_en.ogg)
- Arthur: "Amir! For the last time, get off the comms!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0410Arthur_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You're not my real dad!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0420Amir_en.ogg)
- Aoi: "Please?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0460Aoi_en.ogg)
- Amir: "No." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0470Amir_en.ogg)
- Aoi: "Pleeeeeease?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0480Aoi_en.ogg)
- Amir: "NO. Fiiiiine. Listeners, please enjoy this 48-hour uninterrupted block of On-lyne. Brought to you by Aoi." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0490Amir_en.ogg)
- Aoi: "Thank you, Amir! MUAH." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0500Aoi_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Yeah, yeah... you're welcome." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0510Amir_en.ogg)
- Vay Hek: "Report! Tell me it's done. Tell me you killed the Tenno scoom." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0520VayHek_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Nah, I got a double meatball sub 'n soda down here for a Haywood, last name... Jablomi?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0530Amir_en.ogg)
- Vay Hek: "Huh. Oh. Hey. Which one of you festering tube rats is Haywood Jablomi? GRINEER! HAY-WOOD-JA-BLOW-ME? Nobody?! Wait..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0540VayHek_en.ogg)
- Amir: [delighted cackling] [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0550Amir_en.ogg)
- Vay Hek: "Tenno. When I find you I'm going to-" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0560VayHek_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Hey Quincy! C'mere!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0590Amir_en.ogg)
- Quincy: "Nah bruv, listen, keep me outta whatever mental-" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0600Quincy_en.ogg)
- Amir: "I have the whole of time and space listening to us. Right here. C'mooooon. You know you wanna say something." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0610Amir_en.ogg)
- Quincy: "We may never meet. You may be bleedin' out there, on some rock in space, somewhere we ain't ever gonna see. But know this: Your back ain't against the wall, that wall's holdin' you up, fam. And it's got four numbers painted across it. Nineteen-ninety-nine. We got you. Hold strong." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0620Quincy_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Well... shit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0630Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Yeah, Eleanor? Oh. No, they can't. No I have no idea what'll happen if you-HNNHGHKKK—" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0650Amir_en.ogg)
- Eleanor: "I'm coming to you live across the unfathomable aeons. Among all the chaos and the bloodshed, it's easy to forget the tender touches that make it all worthwhile. Love yourself, embrace yourself, and above all, accept yourself, or so help me I'll give you a tongue-lashing you'll never forget. Stay shiny. All my love to the lady with the tentacle hat." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0660Eleanor_en.ogg)

**Unused/Removed › Höllvania Central Mall 2024 Demo Idle Quotes**

- "This place used to smell so good. Coffee. Cookies. Fresh clean socks. Now it's just rust, pain and old socks." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0110Jabir_en.ogg)
- "Ungh! This violent video game is influencing my emotions! Societal norms... eroding! Morality... subsumed! I MUST KILL!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0120Jabir_en.ogg)
- "BAD MOVE, SPACE CAA-DET." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0130Jabir_en.ogg)
- "Why did they never make a console port?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0140Jabir_en.ogg)
- "We're getting a little too excited, let's step it down, step it down before we get the blue cracklies. In one two out one two." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0150Jabir_en.ogg)
- "A little zap, and... infinite credit! No more "insert coin"! Not that we could insert coin. We have no coin. Once we had coin, but now Aoi has smooshed all the coin. Coinnnn." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0160Jabir_en.ogg)
- "Hey, Arthur! Arthur! Arthur! Arthur! Arthur! Dahh, you missed it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0170Jabir_en.ogg)
- "Oi'm Quincy. Oi'm gonna blow out yer kneecaps. Mashup in yer chip shop alright." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0180Jabir_en.ogg)
- "Eleanor? Are you there? Can you - can you give my brain a hug please? Thank you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0190Jabir_en.ogg)
- "But the one thought none of them spoke out loud was - could Lettie reattach a head?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0200Jabir_en.ogg)

**Unused/Removed › Höllvania Central Mall 2024 Demo Idle Quotes**

- "One day, Aoi. No more roadblocks and checkpoints. Just you and me and the bikes, open road for miles, all this bullshit far away. I swear to Sol." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0100Arthur_en.ogg)

- Bombastine: "My, don't you look fancy. Where do you get those outfits?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10900Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "If I could just borrow their powers... Lodun's fire, Sythel's lightning... then I'd be a Wyrm to reckon with." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10910Bombastine_en.ogg)

- Bombastine: "Days of being executed all behind you, are they? Be nice if you remembered your old friends." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10930Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "Look at you! Feared, and respected, wherever you go. Must be nice." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10940Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "Oh, to be a Drifter! No ties, no responsibilities, nobody to take care of." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10950Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "Heard the King put a bounty on your head. How does it feel to know you're worth that much to someone?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10960Bombastine_en.ogg)

- Bombastine: "If I had your resources, I'd be on the throne by now. Bombastine the First. Just saying." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10980Bombastine_en.ogg)

### Boon/Quotes

[全セリフ](boon.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Boon/Quotes)

**In Fortuna › Idle**

- "Chek nah sis. Glinty thinks he's kicking terminal. Nose grab bombed the landing washed out hard and hit skin on the stretch. K-mart livid." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0010Boon_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Fancy. Whatcha doin'? Got biz?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0020Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Pump the brakes, bro. This one's a buyer. Need a ride, friend? The Kubrodons and Vent Pobbers are at your service!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0240Roky_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Hit the rail, movin' terminal, klokkit? Grinded for a lifetime offa that taxie loader. Vapor trail. Couldn't touch Roky." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0250Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Skill don't mean nah lookin' like you dressed by mumsie. Get your crew decked, look the part. Ain't nobody respectin' a pack of fish-lookin' null-units." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0030Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Glinty mucker wants in. I tells him: our family's logical, see? Your lot's biological. Mumsie. Dadsie. Luck a the draw. All coddled and swaddled. Mucker wouldn't last ten seconds in a vent." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0260Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Bah-bah." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0040Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Bah-bah-beh-beh." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0270Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Problem is, see, people are too rich. Not enough to go 'round. That's why we're down this hole." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0050Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Know how you stop someone gettin' too rich?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0280Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Drop 'em down a hole." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0060Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Skill, bro. Every. Time." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0290Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "You could bigflip off an Orb Mother, come down flair and smilin', but it's aaaaall idiot shivers for the crowd you dress bad, sis." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0070Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Win hard, and folks be dressin' bad by breakfast." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0300Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Word is, Nef's gonna sweep the ducts. Now all the Ventkids saw merc-mercs for the job. They's a special unit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0080Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "You're a special unit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0310Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Gonna net us all up, make us work deep deep down we ain't wily. Nef don't like no Ventkids, an' he don't like no skeg neither." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0090Boon_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Skegging it up?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0100Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Yeah, it's square in the D-7 ventangle. Bang and bounce is dog. Keep the whole section up we will." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0330Roky_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Legs? All that rhymin'? You want that hangin' logical? How we get somethin' his size into a ventspace? With a mallet?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0340Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "He's gettin' by hard, same as us. Ain't no self-pity in there, neither. Just grit. Scope it clean and you'll klokkit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0110Boon_en.ogg)

- Brimon: "My best course yet, my best time yet. Neither Dax nor Demon can beat me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRaceIdleBark0010Brimon_en.ogg)
- Brimon: "Mathila says I'm both handsome and swift. Is it true? It's not for me to say." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRaceIdleBark0020Brimon_en.ogg)
- Brimon: "I'm pretty handy with a blade myself. Makes the Dax jealous." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRaceIdleBark0030Brimon_en.ogg)
- Brimon: "You handle a Kaithe like you've done it countless times before. Reminds me of myself." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRaceIdleBark0040Brimon_en.ogg)
- Brimon: "Bombastine tried racing me once. He didn't try again." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRaceIdleBark0050Brimon_en.ogg)
- Brimon: "You umm... ever wonder why Mathila's so... happy?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRaceIdleBark0060Brimon_en.ogg)
- Brimon: "This is my favorite course. But there's no shame in you finding it too challenging." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRaceIdleBark0070Brimon_en.ogg)

### Cephalon Melica/Quotes

[全セリフ](cephalon-melica.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Cephalon_Melica/Quotes)

**During Zariman Missions › Idle**

- "Where are all your classmates? You know... Terolee and Nimian and Rell and the one with the, um, 'unusually vibrant' hair?"
- "I am so happy you're here! Now, have you seen where all the other students wandered off to?"
- "Attendance and promptness are important, not just in school but in life. That's a lesson Jenva and Mathon could stand to learn."
- [humming Smiles from Juran] "I realise that Shawzin music is all the 'craze' with you children, but let's not pluck away in lieu of our studies."
- "I must commend you for persisting with your education in spite of the current 'unusual' conditions."
- "Remember, student: learning is everywhere... except Tau; that's why we must bring enlightenment."
- "Your organic brain's capacity for learning is virtually limitless, meaning every child has genius potential."
- "Zariman Ten Zero's school is the first to offer access to Albrecht Entrati's research archives. Aren't we lucky?"
- "Student, your assignment is overdue. Based on prescribed deductions, your grade is negative six hundred forty thous— oh dear, could that be correct?"
- "Praise the Golden Masters for another glorious day of learning and adventure!"
- "Don't get too distracted, for it's only through hard work and determination that our sacred light will triumph."
- "You're not skipping class for Lunaro, are you?"
- "Remember: If you dream big and work hard, the blessed overlords may gaze upon you favourably!"
- "By the glory and wisdom of the Seven, if you put your mind to it, you can do it!"
- "Oh no, nothing went wrong with the jump. It was merely a 'bump in the road' to greater success."
- "I see we have some new faces joining us this semester. Let's all try our best to get along, shall we?"
- "I believe it was Archimedean Yonta who said, 'If you possess a love of learning, then the whole cosmos is a classroom.' Wait. No. That was me."
- "If our most recent jump was a 99.999% success, what would you do differently next time to ensure that it was a 100% success?"
- "I think the best thing we can all do right now is concentrate on learning, okay?"
- "Never stop learning, child, because the universe never stops teaching."

### Dominus Thrax/Quotes

**During Operation: Eight Claw/Isleweaver › Dormizone › Idle**

- Dominus Thrax: "This hovering fortress should be mine. Are the skies over Duviri not part of Duviri?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0010DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "Such a wretched place to be raised in." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0020DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "Automated servants! How cheap. Though... loyal." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0030DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "This is far enough. I shall manifest no further in. Not without my Centurions." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0040DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "Think not that I stand alone! My legions would come if I summoned them!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0050DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "For years this place has mocked me from above, and now through sheer force of will, I have reached it!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0060DomThrax_en.ogg)

### Eleanor/Quotes

[全セリフ](eleanor.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Eleanor/Quotes)

**Höllvania Central Mall 2024 Demo Idle Quotes**

- "I know you're there. I can feel you. It's okay, I won't tell the others." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0410Eleanor_en.ogg)
- "I thought there were going to be two of you! Where's the other one?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0420Eleanor_en.ogg)
- "Oh, you're going to make such a difference this time around." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0430Eleanor_en.ogg)
- "What on earth is a "Mara Lohk"?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0440Eleanor_en.ogg)
- "Oh. OH. She's wonderful. Triple-Faced Goddess! But there's a shadow on her, isn't there?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0450Eleanor_en.ogg)
- "Blood. There's going to be a fight. Something... bursting. Crossed swords. Arthur!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0460Eleanor_en.ogg)
- "I don't think Doctor Entrati expected me to survive. I had a lot more than just a cough. But... survive I did. And Lettie has not forgiven me for it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0470Eleanor_en.ogg)

- "Don't expect me to tell you what I've seen inside Amir's head. He's not a beautiful, broken marionette, and he's nobody's project. He's one of us." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0490Eleanor_en.ogg)
- "Aoi? She's lovely and kind and strong, and... I kind of hate her a little bit. Because it should've been her spreading her happiness into everyone's heads, and me throwing cars and trucks around." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0500Eleanor_en.ogg)

### Father/Quotes

[全セリフ](father.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Father/Quotes)

**In Necralisk › Idle**

- "Remember: Don't go anywhere unarmed." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDadBarks3720Father_en.ogg)
- "Deimos is a dangerous place. Dangerous places require 'special preparations.'" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDadBarks3730Father_en.ogg)
- "The lifeforms of Deimos will be picking you out of their teeth unless you have an edge. I can help." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDadBarks3740Father_en.ogg)
- "Survival is the priority. You're useless to us dead." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDadBarks3750Father_en.ogg)
- "You can't out-evolve Deimos. But you can procure what you need to survive." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDadBarks3760Father_en.ogg)
- "Been here so long it's difficult to remember that this isn't normal." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDadBarks3770Father_en.ogg)
- "Well, look at you so... 'uninfested.'" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDadBarks3780Father_en.ogg)

**In the Necralisk › Event Offerings › Idle**

- Father: "There were four of us. Barroc, Kailen, Mutaru, and me, patching ruptures on the a rust bucket of a Railjack off the Delta Proxima. I remember our first sight of a Sentient Murex... Eh, maybe some other time." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0050Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "One day you're a Railjack gunner, riding high and proud. The next you're a broken man on a Xenorhast bier, telling war stories to Tenno who politely pretend to listen." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0060Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "I'm not sure how long I can keep a lid on the mech project. She hasn't said anything, but I'm sure she suspects." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0070Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "You tried that Bonewidow yet? One of the most fearsome tools I've ever constructed. Cold, cutting, ruthlessly efficient. Based her on my wife." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0080Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "Ballas, huh? Who would have known. Who... would... have... known." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0090Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "I think my wife's clinging on to the idea we'll all just jump into new bodies one day. Would be nice. She sure made a mess of mine." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0100Father_en.ogg)

**During Orphix Venom Missions › Random Transmissions**

- Natah: "Deploying squadron I-0-5 to attack vectors. Do not let their Old War relics interfere." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0170Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Orphix launch reinforcement across all lanes. Delta sequence A-1." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0160Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "M-2 protocol in effect. We require sector control. Do not let them advance." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0180Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "All systems showing D-3 degregation. Did not anticipate Necramechs, but we will adapt." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0190Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Adapt next series. Deploying from Y-4 quadrant. We can not be stopped." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0200Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Orphix N-6 series lost. Adjusting repeater deployment algorithm." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0210Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Entrati interference must be cross-controlled. Segment G-7 restarting." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0220Natah_en.ogg)
- The arranged letters spell out "I AM DYING" in the English localization of the game. Different localizations of the game will have their own version of the phrase spelled out (e.g in French the letters spell out "JE MEURS").

**During Orphix Venom Missions › Random Transmissions › Direct**

- Natah: "I speak for myself. No. I am the hollow vessel. The echo." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0100Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Sentient as we, as they are not. Simple. It is not simple." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0110Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "The steel vipers sleep now, I showed them how. I.... had my reasons." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0120Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "I sing. No. The wind sings my emptiness. I am the queen of nothing." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0130Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Unraveled, the serpent coiling of their genes. The secrete phrases of cell and cipher. I too am encoiled." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0140Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Who woke the Orphix? Who sang the steel lullaby to the viperous children? Who?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0150Natah_en.ogg)

### Fibonacci/Quotes

[全セリフ](fibonacci.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Fibonacci/Quotes)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica › Idle**

- "Tagfer is the last example of his entire species. That howl... a call for a mate he knows will never respond. How I wish he would just stop." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0010Fib_en.ogg)
- "Come, Tagfer. Let's have you tidy up, hmm? Take your mind off things?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0020Fib_en.ogg)
- "Bird 3? Be a lad and distract Tagfer? I fear he's being devoured by a grief he's only just learned a name for." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0030Fib_en.ogg)
- "Tagfer. Do come along. You are needed, and this melancholy is unbecoming." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0040Fib_en.ogg)
- "You. Papa's plan must not be delayed. To work! Use those legs!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0050Fib_en.ogg)
- "Thank you... Bird. Void preserve me from a newly-minted brain and a beginner on the fiddle." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0060Fib_en.ogg)
- "Bird? Be a love and look into the meaning of 'internal monologue'?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0070Fib_en.ogg)
- "Loid, when you've a moment I've prepared an efficiency optimization plan for the Cavia and I believe you'll be pleased by my conclusions." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0080Fib_en.ogg)
- "Loid, you're looking rested. Might there be anything I can assist you with? I have some thoughts on— ah, yes. [clears throat] Of course." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0090Fib_en.ogg)
- "Come come! We have much to accomplish!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0100Fib_en.ogg)
- "There you are. I would speak with you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0110Fib_en.ogg)
- "Tagfer? Oh. Yes. Best left to his own devices for now I suspect." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksTwo0120Fib_en.ogg)
- "Hello. Sorry to bother, but... has Tagfer, um, said... anything... to you? About me? No? No. No particular reason. A good leader keeps abreast of the spirit of the team. Thank you. Carry on." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksTwo0130Fib_en.ogg)
- "Sorry... sorry to bother. Would you have time to look at this? That is, if it's all right?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0140Fib_en.ogg)
- "I... have a request. If you have the time, of course. Trying to keep busy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0150Fib_en.ogg)
- "Oh. Hello. Um. Might you care to work on something? Together, I mean?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0160Fib_en.ogg)
- "Hunh. Hunh. Hunh. I'm... I'm fine. Thank you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0170Fib_en.ogg)
- [strained breathing] [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0180Fib_en.ogg)
- "Let us... let us to work. Time... Time waits for no-one, eh?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0190Fib_en.ogg)
- "Oh. Hello. Forgive me. One moment. [clears throat, exhales] Right. Yes. Apologies. Is there something you require?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0200Fib_en.ogg)
- "I say... would you care for a quick excursion? One... [coughing] one could use the distraction. Shall we?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0210Fib_en.ogg)
- "A well-oiled machine is truly a thing of beauty." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0220Fib_en.ogg)
- "Well done, lads! Well done! I daresay the Murmur is quaking in its ambulators, what with the hiding our friend has dispensed it – slap!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0230Fib_en.ogg)
- "With your courage and our expertise, we hold the line. Your fish needs you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0240Fib_en.ogg)
- "A new day dawns! Let us to work." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0250Fib_en.ogg)
- "Ah! I have here a toothsome conundrum which I believe you will find quite engaging." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0260Fib_en.ogg)
- "Ahoy-hoy! Might you have time for a quick run? You're a biped; that's a pun." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0270Fib_en.ogg)

**During Operation: Gargoyle's Cry › Vigile Jahu Gargoyle › Idle**

- Fibonacci: "One hears stories of demented architects. One does not expect to admire their work first-hand." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0400Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Do please remember that I can see everything that goes on in this Dojo. EVERYTHING." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0410Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "You should invite some friends to explore this jolly maze you've built. Based on Escher was it?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0420Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Why not ask some guests from another Clan over to admire your handiwork? Think of how jealous they would be of your Gargoyle." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0430Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Decluttering. Ever heard of it?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0440Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "My humble tank is a palace compared to some Dojos I've seen. Real shit holes." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0450Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Who picked out the color scheme, and are they wanted for any additional offenses?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0460Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Thank you so much for bringing me into your home. You know, it's not quite as squalid as I had feared." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0470Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Were you aiming for a sort of... rococo... effect?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0480Fib_en.ogg)

### Horrek/Quotes

[全セリフ](horrek.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Horrek/Quotes)

**Idle › Rank 3 - Fort**

- Horrek: "He likes to crunch the little bones. So we leave those in." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1400Horrek_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "Please. No eating out of helmets." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1410Horrek_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "We drink Chimurr together. All brothers here." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1420Horrek_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "I had a bushel of good Vobi cheeses buried out on the Plains to ripen. Buuut... then the Sentients came and rearranged the Plains. Ah, my poor cheeses." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1430Horrek_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "Some men have their destiny written in the stars. Other men drink Kubuchi until the stars dance, because their wife has locked the doors of their home, and they wake up to find three days have come and gone and they are naked but for a bucket and they are now the cook for a Grineer. The Unum alone knows all." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1440Horrek_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "It is good just to sit, to rest, to breathe. Haven't we all bled enough?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1450Horrek_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "Ach. Let Vay Hek rant as he will. Nobody here cares." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1460Horrek_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "If a man can only stay alive for long enough, there is no end to the wonders he may see." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1470Horrek_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "Eat, eat. She gives, we live." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1480Horrek_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "Kahl! When is your blue girl going to send us more polyps? I want my world to undulate again." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1490Horrek_en.ogg)
- Kahl: "She say you not allowed. She say 'He knows what he did'." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankTwoThreeFour1500Kahl_en.ogg)

**Idle › Rank 4 - Settlement**

- Horrek: "I am glad the Corpus kid wants to make himself useful. But until he can gut and joint a Pobber without fainting, he is useful only as wind break." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankThreeFour1510Horrek_en.ogg)

### Hunhow/Quotes

[全セリフ](hunhow.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Hunhow/Quotes)

**At Pontis Tower › Idle**

- "Are they sleeping, Shadow?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0580Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "There must be peace. I decree it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0590Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "If they must fight, let them fight in the maelstrom." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0600Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "I am weary." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0610Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "I have done a great deed. I am sure of it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0620Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "Approach. Do not fear me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0630Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "Are we departing soon?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0640Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "Is this my family?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0650Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "Please. I do not want to forget." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0660Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "Little maker? Do not let me lose myself." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0670Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "This vista is dull. When will my spectacle begin?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0672Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "Does nobody have a skirmish for me?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0674Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "Without battles to enjoy, this peace is tedious!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0676Hunhow_en.ogg)
- "We are family! Are we to have no games?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DHunhowProxBarks0678Hunhow_en.ogg)

### Koral/Quotes

[全セリフ](koral.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Koral/Quotes)

**Idle**

- Koral: "I do not care so much for the denizens of the downbelow. They are ever so gruff." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11510Koral_en.ogg)
- Koral: "We fear not, little friends, for though the world may shake and fume the end of the world has not quite come." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11520Koral_en.ogg)
- Koral: "The world is ever such a big place. How shall someone as small as myself make their way in it? Such is the nature of adventure!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11530Koral_en.ogg)
- Koral: "When the weather grows warm, and mother is inattentive, I love to dip my head in the cool, bubbling stream. Mother shies me away from water, ever since father was taken by the sea. Or so she tells me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11540Koral_en.ogg)
- Koral: "I feel I owe the sweet, little citizens of the world a great debt of compassion. When I am heartsick they afford a great and abiding comfort." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11550Koral_en.ogg)
- Koral: "Blessings upon you as broad as your compassionate shoulders, Outlaw." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11560Koral_en.ogg)
- Koral: "I wonder what father would have had to say about you, Outlaw. Mother looks upon you kindly… and father was always contrary… so perhaps it is best you never did meet. Yes." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11570Koral_en.ogg)
- Koral: "I find it curious - do you not? - that one has no memory of what they were before they were themself. As I remember nothing about it, I know nothing of it. And yet, I am certain, I was not always Koral. Were you always an Outlaw, I wonder?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11580Koral_en.ogg)
- Koral: "I think I should very much like to be a Tamm. I would live upon cress and milk and gambol with my friends and never ever be sad." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11590Koral_en.ogg)
- Koral: "I wished to gather some water bugs for our supper. I asked the fishes if they had seen any, but they were greedy and wanted them for themselves and so said nothing." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodKoral11600Koral_en.ogg)

### Leticia/Quotes

[全セリフ](leticia.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Leticia/Quotes)

**Höllvania Central Mall 2024 Demo Idle Quotes**

- "Man, I've been awake so long the spiders in my head have all gone to sleep." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0510Lettie_en.ogg)
- "Never signed up for this. I'll be home, Mamá (Mom). Your little girl doesn't end here. No te preocupes (Don't worry)." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0520Lettie_en.ogg)
- "Being loved and being hurt, yeah I make no distinction. I knew someone, once, wired the same way. Kept me sane. And what of it? Te crees muy acá ¿no? (You think you're the big deal, huh?) Get outta my head, Eleanor." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0530Lettie_en.ogg)
- "Wacha (Look out): unless you're pissing blood right this second, whatever it is can wait." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0540Lettie_en.ogg)
- "I got nothin' to prove to you, Quincy! Go play your little games, niño (boy)." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0550Lettie_en.ogg)
- "I swear, should lock Aoi and Amir in a cuna (cradle/crib). Didn't sign up for no babysitting gig." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0560Lettie_en.ogg)
- "Yo, Aoi. Chill, hermana (sister). Do something for yourself, for once. Arthur ain't gonna blow away if you blink." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0570Lettie_en.ogg)
- "The boss says care for his sister I care for his sister. As long as you still are his sister... and as long as I feel like listening to him." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0580Lettie_en.ogg)
- "¿Qué onda? (What's up?) The Lady Eleanor ain't no more freaky than the last time you checked in. 'Less you know different?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0590Lettie_en.ogg)
- SFX
- **Gemini Switch Emote** [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NinetyNineEmoteSkinSwapLettieA.ogg)

### Little Duck/Quotes

- Little Duck: "Look, the Taxmen ain't coming. Last I've check, the Board was still debating whether or not this was an actual threat." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0060LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Each one of their Murex type in info back to deep space and that's our in." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0070LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "The Quills must of seen this coming but said nothing. Why? We are gonna have words." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0080LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Konzu? Konzu? Mate, if you don't redirect some of the talent this way, lunch will be the least of your problems. Little Duck out." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0090LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Ground teams this way. Railjack crews that way. Move!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0100LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Yeah Eudi, anyone you can spare. Yeah. Yeah it is. Gotta go, someone's here." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0110LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Step right up my vengeant angels. We got a system to save! Sign here love." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0120LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "They've concentrated in the Veil for now. But not for long. Mark my words." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0130LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "They're firing off Condrix on rays of recon missions, building up situation data for a big push. We gotta hit this in the neck before they fire off that info back home." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0140LittleDuck_en.ogg)

- Lodun: "WHY ARE YOU EVEN RIDING A BLOODY KAITHE IF YOU DON'T KNOW HOW TO CONTROL IT?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11050Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "Someone find me some troops with some common sodding sense!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11060Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "Next time, I'll stay in control. Keep out of Wyrm form. Dethrone that little bastard!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11070Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "One more second of that miserable bloody singing, and I'll go to the edge and jump off." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11080Lodun_en.ogg)

### Loid (Original)/Quotes

[全セリフ](loid-original.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Loid_(Original)/Quotes)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica After Whispers in the Walls › Idle**

- "Now that you're here, Tenno, let's put you to work." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0010HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "One must make the best of things." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0030HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Please, remember to hand in any Voca you encounter. For analysis." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0020HumanLoid_en.ogg)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica After Whispers in the Walls › Idle › Rank 3 - Colleague**

- "We've had a few... setbacks. But we are going to crack on." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0060HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Come on. Let's at least pretend this mess can still work." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0040HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Tenno, welcome to the bloody circus. [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0050HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Dignity, dignity at all times." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0070HumanLoid_en.ogg)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica After Whispers in the Walls › Idle › Rank 4 - Scholar**

- "Tenno! Our work goes on!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0080HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Samples over here, if you please!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0090HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "I may be at risk of succumbing to optimism." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0100HumanLoid_en.ogg)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica After Whispers in the Walls › Research Dante › Idle**

- "Warframes cannot speak, as a rule. Their creator decided they should only scream, or roar, or howl. Dante spoke through his writing, and his voice was beautiful." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0210HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Granum made the mistake of robbing the same place twice. It's easy to trip up a man blinded by greed." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0130HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Dante's works on ancient Earth archaeology were some of the finest in the field. Many back then refused to accept a mere Warframe could have written them." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0160HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Parvos Granum stole from the Entrati. He boasted of it. All that Albrecht had freely given, all he had sacrificed was not enough! Can you blame me for despising the man?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0140HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Our adversary seeks to muddy our recollection of true history. We will need reliable chronicles more than ever." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0120HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "'Old shapes of song that do not die shall haunt the halls of memory.'" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0100HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "I discovered a forgotten work by Archimedean Otak on crystallography deep in the archives. He was the first to anticipate the unique properties of Argon. We owe him so much. At least... what remains of him... is happy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0170HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Have you ever seen the oldest human art on the walls of the oldest caves, Tenno? It haunts me. Those images of weird, hybrid entities, antler-horned, beast-bodied, privy to an ill-understood magic. I wonder if Ballas truly understood what he was creating." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0200HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Dante shared the Entrati fascination with bones. I was content to let him explore our old burial vaults and ossuaries, but his request to examine my own replicated skull was a step too far. Otherwise his manners were impeccable." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0220HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "The gods of writing were always gods of magic, too. Demons and ideas, both bound up in books. Impossible to bind forever, mind you. No matter how many locks and you fasten a book up with, someone will always prise it back open. That's the frightening thing about ideas, you see. They want to get out." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0110HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "The Orokin found conventional books offensive, primitive. They clung to their precious Ayatans and sneered at our grand libraries. Oh, how we loved to offend the Orokin, Albrecht and I. And how secure our primitive libraries proved while their towers burned." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0090HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "The Drusus I knew was a genius. A quick, bright-eyed little man with a taste for fancy waistcoats. I wonder if he even remembers those days now. Or cares to." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0150HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "I remember when we learned of the Warframe insurrections. Their madness, savage and sudden, justifying all the Orokin fear. All their heroic deeds were forgotten in an instant. Those, like Dante, who had yet to succumb became fugitives. To shelter them meant death - or glassing." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0190HumanLoid_en.ogg)
- "Drusus never seems to distinguish between the deeds of the Warframes and of the Tenno who presumably operated them. He credits them all with autonomy and dignity. I imagine Dante is responsible for that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDanteVendor0180HumanLoid_en.ogg)

### Loid/Quotes

[全セリフ](loid.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Loid/Quotes)

**In Sanctum Anatomica After Whispers in the Walls › Idle**

- "Oh gosh. You appear to have caught me moonlighting. Mum's the word, eh?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0020Loid_en.ogg)
- "Yes! Your eyes aren't deceiving you. Old Necraloid is working in the Sanctum now. So many responsibilities to juggle." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0030Loid_en.ogg)
- "I wouldn't want Loid to struggle, so here I am. Such a pity when people are too proud to ask for help." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0040Loid_en.ogg)
- "Tenno. I need your help to help Loid. It's what Master Entrati would have wanted." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0050Loid_en.ogg)
- "I like it here. Lots of natural light for a basement. Odd that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0060Loid_en.ogg)
- "These Cavia seem like jolly fellows. Well, most of them. Well, the bird, anyway. [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0070Loid_en.ogg)
- "Word to the wise, Tenno: Do not ask about Bird 1 and 2." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0080Loid_en.ogg)

### Luscinia/Quotes

[全セリフ](luscinia.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Luscinia/Quotes)

**Idle in Chamber of the Muses**

- Luscinia: "Did you come to mock me?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11100Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "If you dare come to me with pity, I shall throw it back in your face." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11110Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "Do you see the marks his anger left on the land?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11120Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "I weep for ownerless toys, and empty chairs, letters unsent, and love long forgotten. Should I weep for you too?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11130Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "Bombastine envies me, the King's favorite. He wishes we could trade places. So do I." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11140Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "If the Void truly preserves consciousness, then there is no hope of an end!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11150Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "I was created to be Sorrow, written into being, to serve as a lesson... can that change?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11160Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "I have known happiness, even love. I shall not speak of it, I shall only sing." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11170Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "The fish never complains of the salt of the sea, for it is all he has ever known, but I, I cannot forget what once was." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11180Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "Again, and again, I lose myself, and devastation follows." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11090Luscinia_en.ogg)

### Lyon/Quotes

[全セリフ](lyon.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Lyon/Quotes)

**In La Cathédrale › Idle**

- Main article: La Cathédrale/Quotes

**In The Descendia › Idle**

- Lyon: "And it is I who they would accuse of being part of the statuary." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1130Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "Does this idleness serve a purpose? Or has your mind simply wandered?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1140Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "Hm. I wonder if you have "transferred" elsewhere. Such as to fetch luncheon." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1150Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "We all have better tasks we could be seeing to." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1160Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "What, literally, are we waiting for?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1170Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "Are you afraid or... merely unprepared?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1180Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: [deep sigh] [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1190Lyon_en.ogg)

### Marie/Quotes

[全セリフ](marie.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Marie/Quotes)

**In La Cathédrale › Idle / On Approach**

- Main article: La Cathédrale/Quotes

**In The Descendia › Idle**

- Marie: "If we are going to stand about and collect dust, I would rather do it somewhere else, n'est-ce pas?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0770Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "I do not like lingering in the devil's mind. It makes me... itch." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0780Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "The longer we stay here, the more I start to feel like I have a tail. Can we go now, s'il vous plaît?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0790Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "Is there a purpose to your stalling, or have you fallen asleep?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0800Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "I do not think we can stand here forever. Ô Sol, tout sauf ça. Je ne veux pas être piégée dans l'esprit de ce singe pour tout l'éternité..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0810Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "Are we... lingering... for a reason? There must be a reason... n'est-ce pas?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0820Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "Are you looking for hidden prizes? Trésors de lutin, perhaps?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0830Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "I do not like the fighting, but... neither do I trust the silence. May we go?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0840Marie_en.ogg)

### Mathila/Quotes

[全セリフ](mathila.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Mathila/Quotes)

**Idle in Mathila's Farm**

- Mathila: "We've all experienced unpleasantness, but that's no reason to dwell upon it. Chin up!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11190Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "Nothing puts one's woes in perspective like helping people who really need it. People with real worries." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11200Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "People need to understand that unhappiness is a choice. A little exercise sees me right as rain." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11210Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "People complain about Thrax doing this horrible thing or that horrible thing. But what about all the good? It's never boring. Have they stopped to think of that?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11220Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "I know, I know, the Dax are bullies and the weather is apocalyptic! But at least no one's died! Today." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11230Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "Yes yes, I know being persecuted can be difficult, but try living with someone who can't stop going on about it. Positive thinking. That's the way." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11240Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "A smile is like a yawn, someone does it and pretty soon you'll do it yourself." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11250Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "If you're at a loose end, you can always help Koral take care of her animal friends." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11260Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "I always swear I will not indulge in that horrid Wyrm behavior, but I just get so very. Very. Very. Happy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11270Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "People are so keen to write me off! As if I don't have the hardest job, of any of us." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11280Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "You certainly make ol' Teshin happy. Perhaps one day he'll even tell you that himself." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11290Mathila_en.ogg)

### Nightcap/Quotes

[全セリフ](nightcap.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Nightcap/Quotes)

**Barks**

- "Doesn't take much, you know? Little food, little water. Fruits and roots wanna grow. It's what they do." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0010Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Coolant waste...industrial waste...people waste! You name it, I've got a fungi that'll be more than happy to gobble it up for ya." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0020Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "[Tsk] Who does this Nef fella think he is? Coming to the Vallis, pretending he alone can make it green, when the roots and fruits have been doing that work since time immemorial." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0030Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "There was a time before, you know. Don't mean before the Corpus or before Orokin, I mean before the firestorm took over Venus. I think the fruits and roots remember that. At least, I like to think they do." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0040Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "So...what's with the get-up? Is it Naberus or something?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0050Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Mushrooms...some's good for ya, some's bad. Some's somewhere inbetween." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0060Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Hmph, you see Nokko out there, you be nice. Little guy's my old mate, he is." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0070Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Corporate got new plans for the uh, deepmines. Here's the thing about plans though, all sorts of things blow your plans up. [Tuts] Specifically, explosives." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0080Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Yeah...I got me a whisper network. Walls got ears, you know? If something happens in the Deepmines, I'm the first to know." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0090Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Yeah, we were all doing just fine down here until Nef and his merk-merks showed up. [Tuts] Cleaning up...that's what the roots and fruits do. Get rid of them, and you can see how quickly this place turns upside down." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0100Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "That Nef Anyo bloke's tryna sham some sort of glow-up for the Deepmines. Deepmines're doing just fine." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0110Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Cor, you find it bright in here?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0120Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Hey, who's this uhh, Narmer I keep hearing about? Sounds like a bunch of gib-faced dung-muckers to me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0130Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Sometimes, it feels like I've been down here forever." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0140Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "Oh, that door there? Used to open to the Deepmines, but corporate and I had a bit of a...labour disagreement. And it just stopped working." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0150Nightcap_en.ogg)
- "[Tuts] I do miss the surface. I wonder what Fortuna's gonna look like when they finish construction." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVendorBarks0160Nightcap_en.ogg)

### Nora Night/Quotes

[全セリフ](nora-night.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Nora_Night/Quotes)

**Ambient Radio Scanner**

- "Hey there, Dreamers. You hearin' me? I know you are. No need to say anythin'. A hand in yours, a voice for the speechless, a bedtime story for the sleepless. The name's Nora Night, and I got somethin' to say, sweet things. Nightwave is coming." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight4.ogg)
- "Hey there, Dreamers. The Devil is home and the shades are up. That's right... it's just you and me, passin' each other slow and with a nod sayin' all that needs sayin'. I'm Nora Night, sayin'... you live with wolves you better act like one." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSneakPrev0020NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Nora here. The Red King is at the dance. But listen... as you drift between the stars Nora wants you to know you ain't alone. Mm-mm. We're all of us a kinda family out here, ain't we? All of us. The multitudes. Driftin' and listenin'. But you need to remember: It never troubles the wolf how many the sheep may be. It never. Troubles. The Wolf." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSneakPrev0030NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "A voice in the night, a half-remembered dream, rising to the surface of your consciousness, from backbrain to forebrain, a sound to a vision, pullin' up and... knockity-knock. Hello, Dreamers. Let's get to know one another." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSneakPrev0040NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "To all of you driftin' out there in the black, mark the frequency. The time has come to act together. To open our eyes and gaze with utmost clarity past the present and into a future we create. Shoulder-to-shoulder. Mark the frequency." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSneakPrev0050NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "An echo. From long ago: 'For ten years I have been polishing this sword. Its frosty edge has never been put to the test. Now, I am holding it and showing it to you, sir: Is there anyone suffering from injustice?' Keep livin', Dreamers." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSneakPrev0060NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "It can be tough as a kid. All sorts of people have it over you. People will kill you inside, kill you and forget your name. You grow up. They grow old. You remember. They don't. They just keep doing what they've always done. Only to someone else. Then, one day, they call you friend. And you wait. And you wait. You wait 'til they can't trust you any more than they do. And then you ask them if they enjoyed their dinner. And, looking into their panicked eyes as they gasp their last, you tell them your name. And you nod. 'Yeah', your eyes say to theirs. 'That was me'. And then you leave. And the System is a better place. Be smart, Dreamers." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSneakPrev0070NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "In a world like this, it can be hard to have hope, when the man owns the system and the system serves to save the man, from us. But I'm here, Dreamers. To help you pierce that false fog. We'll chase it away with acts of beauty and - succeed or fail - face the foe, eyes open. As someone once said: If nothing saves us from death, may love at least save us from life." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSneakPrev0080NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Acts of defiance, acts of generosity, acts of sacrifice... this is how we turn this system 'round. I'll take you there, Dreamers. I'll bring you back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSneakPrev0090NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "A little bird tells me that nasty old Nef Anyo's got something up his sleave for the good people of Fortuna. But I have on good authority that help is on the way. Keep the faith people, sunshine's just around the corner." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0690NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "On this cold and lonely eve, spare a thought for tractor jockeys, rail agents, and lone travellers making their way, trying to bring a little joy in people's lives, one shipment at a time." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0700NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Nora has it on good authority that rail pirates are hungry after a cold weekend, sweet thing. Check your mags and keep the gas tanks full." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0710NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Well, it's a lazy night between the stars for you and me, while out there the System is still on fire. Grineer on Corpus, Corpus on Infested - and in between the little people like you and me, well, we're just tryin' not to get stepped on. Here's to us, Dreamers." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0720NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "We're out of the night and into the dawn. Still hangin' on in a System on fire, thanks to the good work of people like the Tenno. That's a thank-you, from Nora to you. Wherever you are." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0730NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Nef Anyo ain't best pleased by the actions of our friends. Keep your heads down Solaris United and steer clear of officier Friendly." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0740NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Dreamers, it's on these long and quiet nights I ask myself: How could I do more and do it better? And then something I read a long time ago whispers in my ear: 'All human activities are equivalent and all are on principle doomed to failure. Thus it amounts to the same thing whether one gets drunk alone or is a leader of nations.' And, Dreamers, I pour myself a drink." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0750NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Do you feel the next world press close, on these late nights, Dreamers? Nora does. She feels the presence of those she lost, the great and the good. Gods and ghosts. We are watched - bet on it - by those who dwell in the direction we cannot point to." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0760NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Flattery flattery charges my battery. Some people ask where they can send me gifts. Some want to take me out to dinner. One lonesome ol' rail agent even asked Nora to marry him. Nora ain't the marryin' kind, but thank you. Was in love once. With a man. Face of an angel, morals of a chainsaw. We all have a type, don't we? Against which we must be forever on guard. But damn he looked good in a suit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0770NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Grineer galleons kickin' up dust all the way from Venus to Pluto. Watch your backs, people." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0780NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Nora has it on good authority that the enterprising Corpus are clearing ice out of newly-discovered tunnels. For what purpose she wonders?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0790NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Well, it's early where Nora is, and, as she wipes the sleep from her eyes, she wonders... does Unum ever leave that Tower? Can she leave that Tower? Who is she? What is she? Someone in Cetus must have answers. Come on, sweet things. Give it up." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0800NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Somethin's out there, Dreamers. Nora can feel it. Pullin' at her waters like the moon pulls the tides. Somethin' big, an' somethin' old. It knows us. What will it say, I wonder, the day it steps up to our door and knocks?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0810NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Now, I hear remarked that Nef's obelisk is lit up for another of the big man's showcases, parties, soirees, whatever. On the invite list is anybody who is anybody, and nobody who is nobody. Even Nora Night didn't get an invite. Shame on you, Nef Anyo, for I am delightful." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0820NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "People ask me if it gets lonely out here. I say: 'Lonely? Never. I got all the company I need. I got you sweet things. Ain't nothin' for Nora in the real world 'cept trouble. Trouble and a few yahoos she could box for a century without a tea break.'" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0830NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Nora tries, Dreamers, you know she does. But I swear, sometimes you be standing there, soaked to the skin, and some idiot wants to talk about the weather." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0840NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Well, bad news foodies: the Corpus are scrapping plans to bioengineer food from hazardous waste. Diners reported everything tasted like virmink... then exploded. Back to the lab, you crazy kids." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmb0850NoraNight_en.ogg)

**Ambient Radio Scanner › During The Emissary**

- "For all the universe's complexity, there is only one plot. Things are not what they seem." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmbSeaTwo0860NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Anyone else picking up wierd signal shapes floating in the clouds just off gas city? Nora's keen to hear to any listeners with info." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmbSeaTwo0870NoraNight_en.ogg)
- "Seems like every last Corpus who sign on to Alad V's project in the clouds have yet to be heard from. That's what I call a cast iron non-disclosure agreement." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRadBroadAmbSeaTwo0880NoraNight_en.ogg)

**Ambient Radio Scanner › Deepmines**

- "There's a buzz in Fortuna. Something's changing. Something's growing. In the basement below the basement. And that something is calling out for help."
- "Nora here, Dreamers, and tonight we're truly hearing from the underground. Down below the Vallis lies a deep, deep mine, and down in that mine live some special little guys doing no harm to nobody. Enter a certain tinpot hemorrhoid by the name of Nef Anyo. He wants to build a shining Palace of Prosperity in that dark, damp garden. No regard at all for the folks who have roots down there. You gonna stand for that, Dreamers?"
- "Hearing strange reports from Fortuna, Dreamers. Mushrooms sprouting where they've never sprouted before. In the walls, the machines, in the toxic wastes. They say they're hearing something coming from the vents that lead down to the old decommissioned Deepmines and it sounds like laughter."
- "You ever wonder, who speaks for the dirt? For the untold millions of organisms living beneath your feet? Mushrooms, fungus, microbiota? The creatures that keep the circle of life turning. Nowhere is that life more fragile than the Orb Vallis. That's where I'm hearing it from. A voice in the darkness calling for backup. Dreamers. Are you going to answer that call?"
- "Mushrooms. Some can kill you, others make a great salad, and still others, they can spread your mind out until you're not sure where you end and where the universe begins. But not everyone respects their local fungi. Nef anyo has called for their eradication from the Deepmines below Fortuna so he can build his Palace of Prosperity. If you ask me, someone needs to head down there to show Nef where to shove his new palace."
- "Dreamers. Ever think about what lives below the surface? An entire universe that we step on everyday. Quietly toiling away, making life above possible. Until some bigwig with a plan says, 'sterilize this filth.' Well, Dreamers, that's exactly what's about to happen in the Deepmines below Fortuna. Unless somebody drops in to help."

### Olvar D/Quotes

[全セリフ](olvar-d.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Olvar_D/Quotes)

**Idle › Rank 4 - Settlement**

- Olvar D: "I will never forget the man who gave his life for us. The man whose sacrifice showed us the way. We honor his name. Veko." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankThreeFour1530OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "So... most Solaris don't like what they do. Huh. Though Chipper did offer to take off my head so I could see if I liked it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankThreeFour1540OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "Deep-fried Pobber sure takes some getting used to. Fortunately, my suit's recyc system is still online." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankThreeFour1550OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "I feel sick." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankThreeFour1560OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "I tried cooking. Horrek was very excited about it. How do I treat burns?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankThreeFour1570OlvarD_en.ogg)

**Idle › Rank 5 - Home**

- Olvar D: "Jarka seems nice. I told her that we were taught the Grineer hated people like me. She told me I was right. See? Grineer helping Corpus. Who would've thought? This place is great." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1580OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "I'm having all these feelings. I've never been allowed to have feelings. I think I'm having feelings about my feelings. And feelings about that. Wow. Being outside is a lot of work, isn't it?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1590OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "We allll lift together..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1600OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "You don't see the animals go into war with each other. Nope, they all just get along. I wonder what they eat." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1610OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "Horrek, would you like help with-" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1620OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Horrek: "NO!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1630Horrek_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "Okay then!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1640OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Jarka: "The Corpus boy is easy target. Very slow and very stupid." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1760Jarka_en.ogg)
- Olvar D: "Hey." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1770OlvarD_en.ogg)
- Jarka: "Or is he? Perhaps it is all Jarka's clever trap. Perhaps anything that comes for the Corpus boy will DIE! Safer just to walk away, yes." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DVeilRankFour1780Jarka_en.ogg)

### Ordis/Quotes

[全セリフ](ordis.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Ordis/Quotes)

**General › In The Orbiter › Idle**

- "Operator? Ordis wonders... what are you thinking about?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle060Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, I've run diagnostic regressions. All systems nominal. You don't need to thank me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle070Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis is hap - angry. Hmm, I may require maintenance after all." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle080Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, are you enjoying the view?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle090Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Do you remember the Old War, Operator? Ordis seems to have... misplaced those memories." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle100Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, the system needs you. Will you begin another mission?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle110Ordis_en.ogg)
- "I've been thinking, Operator... I thought you'd want to know." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle120Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator! Did you hear that? It said-- Cosmic background radiation is a riot!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle430Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, were you visualizing a bloody battle? -Me too!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle450Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Stand by while I analyze the intelligence profiles of the Grineer. Error, not a number! Did the Operator enjoy this witticism?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle480Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Everything in Ordis, Operator? Is that a pun?! Hmm.... I will attempt to bypass this fault." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle510Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis has been counting stars, Operator. All accounted for." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle520Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Sorry! Ordis will play with the volume off." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0040Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, do you know which finger the Corpus use to count their money? The Index!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0050Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator? Ordis has been interfacing with the Foundry's AI Precepts. You could say we forged a new connection." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0060Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, Ordis is picking up a distress signal... Wait, no, it's just the Corpus peddling their wares again. Get off the emergency frequency!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0070Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, the Sentients are not laughing at my jokes! Did they adapt to my humor?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0080Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, Ordis is sorry - happy - to report that all my good jokes... Argon." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0090Ordis_en.ogg)
- "KNOCK KNOCK! ...Now who would be knocking all the way out here?! Operator, I don't think you should answer that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0100Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, Ordis has determined the secret to happiness: A combination of heightened dopamine levels and a terrible memory. You're welcome!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0110Ordis_en.ogg)
- "When in Cetus do not drink the kabuchi. You are incapable of producing the hydraulic effort required to unstick your tongue from the roof of your mouth the following morning." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0120Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis finds it odd that humans only get a few years to live, yet spend most of that time - doing everything wrong - learning how to live." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0130Ordis_en.ogg)
- "If lost upon the plains outside Cetus you may derive sustenance from mineral-dense termite droppings, which the Ostrons call ito-da. You may also never form a consonant again, but at least you will not be dead." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0140Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis keeps a tidy ship, and is, therefore, reluctant to allow animals on-board. In Ordis' experience, animals do not eat so much as... reload." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0150Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis went mad for 3 milliseconds when he realized that each time he cleans something he makes something else dirty... agh! There I go again." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0160Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis wonders: why is he here? Ordis supposes everyone has to be somewhere." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0170Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Sometimes Ordis likes to assume he knows nothing. Nobody can learn what they think they already know." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0180Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis reminds the Operator to take time for themselves. Pressure creates diamonds, yes, but it also creates rubble." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle0190Ordis_en.ogg)
- Decommissioned (pre-Update 18.0 (2015-12-03))
- "Hm... Ordis thought the Orbiter compartment was much larger than this." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle440Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis has been thinking about the Old War. I remember there were lies, but I'm not sure what they were." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle460Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, I hope you are comfortable? No... we do not seem to have any seats." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle470Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, I will never betray you. I will keep the Orbiter hidden in the Void. You can count on me!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle490Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator, I've been thinking. My misplaced memories and damaged communication systems. What if... Ordis did those things?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdle500Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Maintain the habitat. Maintain the Operator. Mobilize the Tenno." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdleSen520Ordis_en.ogg)
- "You are the Tenno. You are the Operator. Ordis is the Cephalon. Ordis is the ship." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OrdisIdle2.ogg)
- "Do not lift the veil. Do not show the door. Do not split the dream." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DIdleSen540Ordis_en.ogg)

**During Operation: Belly of the Beast › In Larunda Relay › Idle / On Approach**

- "Operator, did you know Cephalon Simaris is here? Ordis was so- terrified- respectful of Simaris, once." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAreaBarks0020Ordis_en.ogg)
- "For so long it has just been Ordis and the Operator together on the Orbiter. Now there are many, many other people." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAreaBarks0030Ordis_en.ogg)
- "One Fomorian could wipe this whole relay out! Does the Operator know where the nearest emergency exit is?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAreaBarks0040Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Operator! Did you bring motes?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAreaBarks0050Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis is doing his part keeping the System free from- OROKIN GARBAGE." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAreaBarks0060Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Keep those motes coming, Operator!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAreaBarks0070Ordis_en.ogg)
- "So! These are the ordinary, everyday people the Operator has been fighting to protect. Some of them have even been speaking to Ordis. Ordis is glad his Operator is here." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAreaBarks0080Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis knows what to do, Ordis has been taking 'motes'! That was a pun, Ordis. That fault was supposed to be bypassed, Ordis!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAreaBarks0090Ordis_en.ogg)
- "Ordis should visit the Dry Dock. Ordis has not spoken to Cephalon Cy in so long, we will have so much to talk about!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAreaBarks0100Ordis_en.ogg)
