### Pip/Quotes

[全セリフ](pip.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Pip/Quotes)

**Idle near his house at Lonesome Outlook**

- "To the Void with you, Fergin! The King says I can play as loud as I like! What I wouldn't give to hear his grumpy old voice again." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11400Pip_en.ogg)
- "'You ever do requests? You do? Go to the edge and jump!' That's what he'd say. Every day." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11420Pip_en.ogg)
- "You ever gonna make that move? He's never coming back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11410Pip_en.ogg)
- "Real exciting game you got going on over there! He was there every day. Then one day, he... just wasn't." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11450Pip_en.ogg)
- "'Komi is for true Orokin! Shawzin's nothing but a Dax toy!' Ah, he was full of himself." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11430Pip_en.ogg)
- "'Shuddup,' he would say. 'Who taught you to play, a kaithe's arse?' Now all I hear is this shawzin." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11500Pip_en.ogg)
- "What was that, Fergin? Can't hear you over the crowds! I keep thinking I'm gonna look up and see him, scowling across at me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11460Pip_en.ogg)
- "This one's for you, Fergin! He hated my music. Hated me, too. Void, I miss him." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11490Pip_en.ogg)
- "You know what he'd say to me? 'You think you're making Father proud? Playing shawzin all day?' Like I was nothing but a loafer." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11440Pip_en.ogg)
- "Oh really? Why don't you come over here and say that to my FACE? I know just what he'd say to that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11480Pip_en.ogg)
- "Ha! I've got an audience! I don't see anyone lining up to play Komi with you! Every day, it feels like a little bit more of this place is lost." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11470Pip_en.ogg)

### Quincy/Quotes

[全セリフ](quincy.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Quincy/Quotes)

**Höllvania Central Mall 2024 Demo Idle Quotes**

- "Thassit (That's it)... nice and steady." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0310Quincy_en.ogg)
- "Don't look up, Doctor." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0320Quincy_en.ogg)
- "You don't know me. Never see what darkens your rooftops. Inevitable, like the rain. Handing out consolations in a transient connection. Boom. Smoke. And ghost." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0340Quincy_en.ogg)
- "Don't mind the waiting. Plenty to be thinking about." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0350Quincy_en.ogg)
- "Arthur needs to leave the Major to me, innit. Respect my methods." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0360Quincy_en.ogg)
- "Amir is a weak, weak boy. Little Aunty said, "duppy know who fi frighten. (a ghost knows who to scare)"" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0370Quincy_en.ogg)
- "How many man have the opps got? Not enough t'be takin' me. Never." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0380Quincy_en.ogg)
- "You wiv (with) me, Eleanor? How deep in you go? See anythin' you fancy, girl?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0390Quincy_en.ogg)
- "Oi, Lettie! Grab y' strap (Grab your gun (slang)) and let's go! Best a five buys the drinks?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0400Quincy_en.ogg)
- SFX
- **Gemini Switch Emote** [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NinetyNineEmoteSkinSwapQuincyA.ogg)

### Railjack/Crew/Quotes/Arbiters of Hexis

[全セリフ](railjack-crew-arbiters-of-hexis.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Railjack/Crew/Quotes/Arbiters_of_Hexis)

**On Railjack › Idle**

- "We tread the selfsame Void where the saga began. In the spaces between, we shall learn the truth. All is unfolding as it should." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2920RJAHMale_en.ogg)
- "Let me be worthy of this great office. If I fail, I shall gaze unblinking into the core until my eyes are burned away. I swear it by the Zariman." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2930RJAHMale_en.ogg)
- "The pain of truth is but the smarting of a cleansed wound. Those who stopped up the truth in themselves are like one impacted with his own filth." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2940RJAHMale_en.ogg)
- "Never - make light of our duty to kill when necessary - in service - to the truth. A judgment written in blood - may never - be unwritten." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2950RJAHMale_en.ogg)
- "Many shall ask: by what right do you judge us? And we shall answer: by what right do you question us?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2960RJAHMale_en.ogg)
- "It is no sin to fail to see the truth. But to reject the truth once it is seen? That is sin unpardonable." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2970RJAHMale_en.ogg)

### Railjack/Crew/Quotes/Cephalon Suda

[全セリフ](railjack-crew-cephalon-suda.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Railjack/Crew/Quotes/Cephalon_Suda)

**On Railjack › Idle**

- "Observation. This vessel is golden age Orokin tech, restored to full functionality. Secondary power systems appear to be Orgone-based! Fascinating." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle1480RJCSFem_en.ogg)
- "Voicelog. To ease the separation from Cephalon Suda, I've started a friendly discourse with the ship cephalon. I genuinely think he likes me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle1490RJCSFem_en.ogg)
- "We have too many weapons. I propose replacing a turret with a tachyon wave analyzer. Significant data returns May be anticipated." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle1500RJCSFem_en.ogg)
- "Field research is far more stimulating than data aggregation. My compatriots at the Suda hub would give their internal organs to be here." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle1510RJCSFem_en.ogg)
- "I observe some of your kind develop neck cysts. Why is this? Do you ever need help to pop them? Is it possible to catch the Infestation from a Warframe?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle1520RJCSFem_en.ogg)
- "I have taken the liberty of drilling samples from the hull for analysis. I assure you, there is no cause for alarm. Cephalon Suda gave her full authorization." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle1530RJCSFem_en.ogg)

### Railjack/Crew/Quotes/New Loka

[全セリフ](railjack-crew-new-loka.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Railjack/Crew/Quotes/New_Loka)

**On Railjack › Idle**

- "Mother bless our Captain, and keep safe from all corruption beyond the firmament. Mother bless our crew, Mother keep our provisions free from worm and mold. Mother bless Cephalon Cy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle3640RJNLFem_en.ogg)
- "I'm getting used to it here. I can't smell recycled air any more, and the lights hardly ever give me a headache." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle3650RJNLFem_en.ogg)
- "This all seems like a fever dream. Maybe it is. Perhaps I'm still at home, in my bed, safe on Earth. How do you live like this?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle3660RJNLFem_en.ogg)
- "I was going to bring my little purebred Kavat on board. Ship's cat. Good luck. But then I thought, what if there's a breach and he gets sucked out? I'm sure Ticker will take fine care of him until I get back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle3670RJNLFem_en.ogg)
- "There are more cells in my body than there are galaxies in the universe. Eight hundred billion more. Figures like that help keep one's ego in check." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle3680RJNLFem_en.ogg)
- "Take the time to stop - and listen - to your heart beat. And thank the Universe for making you. I assume there's a heart somewhere inside all of... that?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle3690RJNLFem_en.ogg)

### Railjack/Crew/Quotes/Red Veil

[全セリフ](railjack-crew-red-veil.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Railjack/Crew/Quotes/Red_Veil)

**On Railjack › Idle**

- "House Zetki were artists. When they killed, they killed with finesse. Purpose. Just like me. This system's rotten, and me? I'm a scalpel." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2200RJRVMale_en.ogg)
- "Fact is, every stable foundation is built on meat and blood. Now most people: they pretend it ain't so. It is not to their taste. Me? Hell. Me: I am one hardcore carnivore. For the greater good, course." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2210RJRVMale_en.ogg)
- "Red Veil do the things most people can't. Without that nothing survives. No cost too great, no blood to precious is what we say. As long is ain't mine." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2220RJRVMale_en.ogg)
- "Torture someone long enough they'll confess to anything. That's my favorite thing about it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2230RJRVMale_en.ogg)
- "So. That Helminth thing you mentioned? You ever fed anyone to it?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2240RJRVMale_en.ogg)
- "Know what I love about this ship? Everyone pulls their weight. I been on Veil ships where we had freeloaders. One guy got so damned indolent I had to eat the bastard." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle2250RJRVMale_en.ogg)

### Railjack/Crew/Quotes/Steel Meridian

[全セリフ](railjack-crew-steel-meridian.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Railjack/Crew/Quotes/Steel_Meridian)

**On Railjack › Idle**

- "I still hear them calling us to obey. Oh, I want to—very badly. Always ends with a bulkhead dented and my hand busted. Disobedience isn't a choice I only make once. It's a fah-sukh-ung lifestyle. And it hurts." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0030RJSMFem_en.ogg)
- "Yes we eat our fallen brothers, we live short; kids, family, not for us, if Grineer live on it's through our brothers and our sisters by helping them to live after we have fallen. So shelve your judgment. Luxury makes me nauseous." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0040RJSMFem_en.ogg)
- "There are others out there like me going it alone against everything they were created to be, knowing they're different, knowing why, knowing they'll never belong to anything ever again, I'm lucky, but I have a hard time living with that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0050RJSMFem_en.ogg)
- "Piece of advice, any of the crew wanna spend some private time together just let it happen, play some music, go for a walk, whatever. Don't play the hard ass we're all grownups and life's too short." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0060RJSMFem_en.ogg)
- "We used to pass the time sniffing spent ammo casings *sniff* sometimes I'll decap a round just to get a whiff of musky vordite base with sharp Rubedo notes and a toasty magnesium finish. I'm kidding we never had fun." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0070RJSMFem_en.ogg)
- "I did bad things, if I don't pay for my actions, I don't own myself, so here I am with you settling my tab, I may have been born a clone but I sure as hell ain't dying as one." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0080RJSMFem_en.ogg)

### Railjack/Crew/Quotes/The Perrin Sequence

[全セリフ](railjack-crew-the-perrin-sequence.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Railjack/Crew/Quotes/The_Perrin_Sequence)

**On Railjack › Idle**

- "You don't have to learn the other guy's language if all you want to do is plant an axe in his head. But trade? That means communication. And once you understand what he's saying, you realize you've got more in common than you thought." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0750RJPSMale_en.ogg)
- "I always get a little jittery going into the Void. Keep thinking I'm going to see Parvos Granum looking at me through the window. Or something even worse." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0760RJPSMale_en.ogg)
- "People used to think coins were sacred. They put the faces of Gods on them. You could tell the future with them if you knew how. If you hold a Granum Crown in your fist, you'll notice it always stays cold. What's that tell you?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0770RJPSMale_en.ogg)
- "Sorry. I was daydreaming. Thinking of all that space out there and the amazing people in it that we can talk to. Myconians, Cephalons, the Entrati... even Sentients, maybe, one day. We've just got to believe." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0780RJPSMale_en.ogg)
- "You know I expected space to be a lot more empty. Hopping pobbers have we been filling it up with stuff. I guess that's what happens when you build machines that can make other machines." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0790RJPSMale_en.ogg)
- " Ergo Glast is what you call a true philanthropist. He could have had it all! But a little thing called “conscience” tripped him up. Did I tell you I almost spoke to him once?” [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJCrewIdle0800RJPSMale_en.ogg)

### Roky/Quotes

[全セリフ](roky.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Roky/Quotes)

**In Fortuna › Idle**

- "Klok it, we got a buyer. Howzit?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0230Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Fancy. Whatcha doin'? Got biz?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0020Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Pump the brakes, bro. This one's a buyer. Need a ride, friend? The Kubrodons and Vent Pobbers are at your service!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0240Roky_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Hit the rail, movin' terminal, klokkit? Grinded for a lifetime offa that taxie loader. Vapor trail. Couldn't touch Roky." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0250Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Skill don't mean nah lookin' like you dressed by mumsie. Get your crew decked, look the part. Ain't nobody respectin' a pack of fish-lookin' null-units." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0030Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Glinty mucker wants in. I tells him: our family's logical, see? Hand-picked. Your lot's biological. Mumsie. Dadsie. Luck a the draw. All coddled and swaddled. Mucker wouldn't last ten seconds in a vent." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0260Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Bah-bah." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0040Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Bah-bah-beh-beh." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0270Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Problem is, see, people are too rich. Not enough to go 'round. That's why we're down this hole." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0050Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Know how you stop someone gettin' too rich?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0280Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Drop 'em down a hole." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0060Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Skill, bro. Every. Time." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0290Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "You could bigflip off an Orb Mother, come down flair and smilin', but it's aaaaall idiot shivers for the crowd you dress bad, sis." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0070Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Win hard, and folks be dressin' bad by breakfast." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0300Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Word is, Nef's gonna sweep the ducts. Now all the Ventkids saw merc-mercs for the job. They's a special unit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0080Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "You're a special unit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0310Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Gonna net us all up, make us work deep deep down we ain't wily. Nef don't like no Ventkids, an' he don't like no skeg neither." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0090Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Vent Pobbers got new digs. An old filtration chamber they don't use anymore! Moving our racks and gear in this cycle!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0320Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "Skegging it up?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0100Boon_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Yeah, it's square in the D-7 ventangle. Bang and bounce is dog. Keep the whole section up we will." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0330Roky_en.ogg)
- Roky: "Legs? All that rhymin'? You want that hangin' logical? How we get somethin' his size into a ventspace? With a mallet?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRokyAmbient0340Roky_en.ogg)
- Boon: "He's gettin' by hard, same as us. Ain't no self-pity in there, neither. Just grit. Scope it clean and you'll klokkit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBoonAmbient0110Boon_en.ogg)

**In Fortuna › Assembling K-Drive › While Idle**

- "I dunno, Boon. I think maybe they're havin' one of them, wotsit, flashbacks. Oi! Glinty! You in there?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRockyBuildIdle0420Roky_en.ogg)
- "Yeah, I know, but we need the credits." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRockyBuildIdle0430Roky_en.ogg)
- "So, ah... you don't make a lot of snap decisions, I guess?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRockyBuildIdle0440Roky_en.ogg)
- "Still decinin'? Fair call, fair call. You don't want the balance to be off, or the plank to be flyin' out from under ya." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRockyBuildIdle0450Roky_en.ogg)
- "Need any advice?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRockyBuildIdle0460Roky_en.ogg)
- "The combo of bits give the plank its personality. Kinda like gettin' married. Not that I ever will. Bachelor for life, me. Married to me art." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaRockyBuildIdle0470Roky_en.ogg)

### Rude Zuud/Quotes

[全セリフ](rude-zuud.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Rude_Zuud/Quotes)

**In Fortuna › Idle**

- "Kitguns! Get your Kitguns! (binary chatter) I'm a craftswoman, not some street corner hackster. (binary chatter) Fine. GUNS GUNS GUNS GUNS GUNS GUNS GUNS! Happy?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaZuudBarks0110Zuud_en.ogg)
- "I was up all night making this crap for you ingrates. Buy something so I get some sleep. Kitguns, get master crafted Kitguns, better than you ding-dongs deserve. Kitguns!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaZuudBarks0120Zuud_en.ogg)
- "Alright you punks! Kitguns, blasters, plasma throwers, whatever you need to do horrible things to horrible people! I could've been an architect, an engineer, a doctor, but no. I'm here selling mechanized disarticulation to roving killers. Mother would be so proud. Kitguns! Get your mucking Kitguns!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaZuudBarks0130Zuud_en.ogg)

### Ryoku/Quotes

[全セリフ](ryoku.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Ryoku/Quotes)

**As Railjack Crew › Idle**

- "Do not tarry." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0110Ryoku_en.ogg)
- "Idleness is weakness." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0120Ryoku_en.ogg)
- "Why do we wait?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0130Ryoku_en.ogg)
- "Meditation has its value. So does action." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0140Ryoku_en.ogg)
- "Unnatural engines... beat within us all." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0150Ryoku_en.ogg)
- "Does this pause serve a purpose? Or are you merely indecisive?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0160Ryoku_en.ogg)
- "I hope you are preparing for our next battle, and not merely counting seconds." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0170Ryoku_en.ogg)
- [sighs] [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0180Ryoku_en.ogg)
- "If you have no need of me...?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0190Ryoku_en.ogg)
- "You test my patience for a reason, I presume." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0200Ryoku_en.ogg)

### Smokefinger/Quotes

[全セリフ](smokefinger.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Smokefinger/Quotes)

**In Fortuna › Idle**

- "I possess tools of pedology and mineralogy. Pursuits to make a person very wealthy indeed. Wealth of intellect and pocket. Treasures of curiosity, treasures of coin." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaSmokeBark0010Smokefinger_en.ogg)
- "Treasures await release from stone and soil. A most fulfilling career for one so minded. Might that be you? Mining, resources and more." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaSmokeBark0020Smokefinger_en.ogg)
- "Prospecting! Quarrying! Excavating! A marvelous fantastic vocation into which a young person might channel their energies. Creating a life that is in every way superior." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaSmokeBark0030Smokefinger_en.ogg)

### Son/Quotes

[全セリフ](son.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Son/Quotes)

**In Necralisk › Idle**

- "The atmo's pulsing today, friend. Just smell those pheromones. Bring me back a little something, what do you say?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSonBarks8030Son_en.ogg)
- "Hey, friend. Round up some Infested for the ingested, if you wouldn't mind? I’d do it myself, but... I'm physically inconvenienced. Just mind the Predasites. Like any good antibody, they'll take you out if they can." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSonBarks8040Son_en.ogg)
- "Oh. Hey. Heading for the ventricle? If you find an interesting sample or two out in the chamber – say, a Vulpaphyla or an Avichaea – I'd love a closer look. I can kit you out so it's a more pleasurable experience." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSonBarks8050Son_en.ogg)
- "Am I to understand from your swagger that you intend to venture out there and bring something exotic in here? Excellent. Let's talk equipment." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSonBarks8060Son_en.ogg)

**In Necralisk › Predasite/Vulpaphyla Revivication › Idle**

- "With mutagens and antigens, I can restore an injured speciment to complete health. There will be side effects however." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSonPetBuildIdlePet8570Son_en.ogg)
- "Both the Predasite and the Vulpaphyla are formidable allies. Just be sure you are up to the task of raising one decently before you commit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSonPetBuildIdlePet8580Son_en.ogg)
- "There's no hurry. The Infestation won't overwhelm this creature now. Its fate is in your hands now." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSonPetBuildIdlePet8590Son_en.ogg)

### Sythel/Quotes

[全セリフ](sythel.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Sythel/Quotes)

**Idle in her home**

- Sythel: "We're both more dangerous than we look, you and I. Keep your distance." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11300Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Were you followed?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11310Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Keep your hands where I can see them." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11320Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "You don't know me. You have no idea what I am capable of!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11330Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Beetles, in the earth, biting oh-so-gently. Tunneling. Up through my skin." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11340Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "I've seen them. The black-eyed children. Pointing at me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11350Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "The first scholar looked into the Void and he feared it. And his fear took shape. Do you understand? That's how all this started." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11360Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "I- I can't stay in here forever. I'll starve, get bitten, catch the swarming rot. Little eggs under my eyelids!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11370Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Listen! There! Did you hear it? Rap. Tap. Tap." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11380Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Not safe here. They'll find me! Her with her carriage. Always nosing around. Got to move, find a new place!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11390Sythel_en.ogg)

### Tagfer/Quotes

[全セリフ](tagfer.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Tagfer/Quotes)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica › Idle › Neutral Rank**

- (sobbing) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0020Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "The bell... everything that happened? I wish I could remember. But it's all just... flashes. Horrible, horrible flashes." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0030Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Sorry. I'm... not very good company right now." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0040Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Oh. Hello. Don't mind me. Just... thinking? I don't like it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0050Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Oh. Sorry. I... I didn't see you there. Can I... help you?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0060Tagfer_en.ogg)
- (Mating call) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0070Tagfer_en.ogg)
- (Mournful lowing) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0080Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "What do I do? What do I do?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0090Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Minn... it should have been me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0100Tagfer_en.ogg)
- (Whimper) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0110Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Pull it together. Do it for her. Pull it together." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksZero0120Tagfer_en.ogg)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica › Idle › Rank 1 - Assistant**

- "There's something wrong with the story? Of what happened to us? Something off. Something... fishy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0130Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Fibonacci seems to have taken to this whole situation quite well? Better than Bird and I, at least. What do you think of him? Fibonacci, I mean." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0140Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "You're human, like Loid. Like... Papa? Does Papa have a plan? Do you think? That, someday, we'll look back and see that... this was all worth it?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0150Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Bird... Bird 3's... sweet? I think my... mood... is scaring him. But I don't what to say, how to tell him how this feels." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0160Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Loid doesn't think much of us, does he? What am I even doing here?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksOne0170Tagfer_en.ogg)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica › Idle › Rank 2 - Researcher**

- "I think I'm starting to understand how brains work. The things you remember, the things you don't... until someone says something that brings it all rushing back..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksTwo0180Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "You need something?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksTwo0190Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "I feel sorry for Bird. He deserves better than to be stuck with us. [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksTwo0200Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Do you think... do you think Loid knew? The bell I mean. What would happen. To us. Down there. Do you think Papa told him?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksTwo0210Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Do you think Loid stood by, while Papa pulled the lever? On those screens... do you think he watched?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksTwo0220Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Do you think Papa saw the moment Minn became a person? The exact same moment she realized she was going to die? The only moment I ever really had with her?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksTwo0230Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "I like you. You're not like the others here. Are you?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksTwo0240Tagfer_en.ogg)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica › Idle › Rank 3 - Colleague**

- "nnnnnnnGAAH!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0260Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "I should have killed him!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0270Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "I can't keep feeling like this. I can't keep feeling like this. I gotta... I gotta get it out. Minn. Minn... what do I do? What do I do?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0280Tagfer_en.ogg)
- (Labored breathing) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0290Tagfer_en.ogg)
- (Coughs) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0300Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "What would you have told me to do? I don't know. I never met the person you became. You existed only for an instant and then... gone." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0310Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "The voices in the walls.. are they getting closer?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0320Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Maybe, those voices in the walls will solve all this for us." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksThree0330Tagfer_en.ogg)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica › Idle › Rank 4 - Scholar**

- "The fish has been on his best behavior. Can't tell if he's really sorry, or just scared. Mm... What can I help with?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0340Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Loid's been less condescending since Bird gave that speech. I'm still gonna kill Albrecht, though. If Loid makes the mistake of saving me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0350Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "That Bird... not as dumb as the fish thinks. As far as I can see... he's the only one... really coming to grips... with what we are now. Sorry. Short of breath. Better now." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0360Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "The Murmur... the 'Great Indifference'. It doesn't seem so indifferent to me. Seems to me it knows exactly what it wants." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0370Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Let's tackle a Netracell. Give me something to focus on other than my woes." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0370Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Wanna hit a Netracell? Focusing seems to be good for me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0390Tagfer_en.ogg)
- (Coughs) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0400Tagfer_en.ogg)
- (Pained lowing) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFour0410Tagfer_en.ogg)

**In the Sanctum Anatomica › Idle › Rank 5 - Illuminate**

- "Yeah. Love you too, baby." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0420Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "You in the mood for a little Netracell action?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0430Tagfer_en.ogg)
- (Happy lowing) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0440Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "I was thinking: maybe we can be de-volved. Back to what we were. Maybe... maybe I could live with that. Simplicity, y'know? Nothing complicated upstairs. No demons." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0450Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Bird 3. What I wouldn't give for some of that optimism." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0460Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Another day another Netracell another poke in the eye for the motherhugger that consigned us to this mess. C'mon. Let's roll." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0470Tagfer_en.ogg)
- "Murmur or no Murmur, I've been sleepin' better since comin' back from the dead. Try it sometime." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarksFive0480Tagfer_en.ogg)

### The Business/Quotes

[全セリフ](the-business.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/The_Business/Quotes)

**In Fortuna › Idle**

- "Offworlder, I'm in the business of keeping you alive. Your quarry, too! Tranq rifles and lures. Help me preserve the miracle of the Orb Vallis ecosystem." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBizBarks0010TheBusiness_en.ogg)
- "Looking to trap Stover? Well, they'll take a piece out of you, you come at them the wrong way. Virmink? Slippery little buggers, but we can fix that, don't you worry." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBizBarks0020TheBusiness_en.ogg)
- "Intending to spend time out in the Vallis? Oh. it's a rare miracle, but death to the unwary. Tell me what you're fixing on, and I'll steer you true." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBizBarks0030TheBusiness_en.ogg)
- "So you want to help preserve this bio-sphere's unique fauna? Hmm, admirable. So, let's talk instrumentation." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaBizBarks0040TheBusiness_en.ogg)

### Ticker/Quotes

[全セリフ](ticker.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Ticker/Quotes)

**In Fortuna › Idle**

- "Hey there Stardust. Our stock is hot, hot, hot, right now. Mmm, mmm!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaTickerBarks0040Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Let's go lovely, chippity-chop!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaTickerBarks0050Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Hello, hello, hello! Old things looking for a new home at very reasonable prices. Under less stressful conditions." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaTickerBarks0070Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Every trinket a story! A story waiting to be shared, my lovely. A story waiting to be freed." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaTickerBarks0030Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Get over here doll. Pre-loved and second-hand. Treasures that deserve a second chance." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaTickerBarks0010Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Odds and ends need love too, sweet thing. Let's you and Ticker talk about givin' something that once knew love a second lease on life." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaTickerBarks0020Ticker_en.ogg)
- "The once-loved in need of a little TLC, Stardust. I hope you came here with credits and good intentions." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DFortunaTickerBarks0060Ticker_en.ogg)

**Idle**

- "We've got two families: biological and logical. Roky taught me that one. Star Day is for both those families... and anyone else besides." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0040Ticker_en.ogg)
- "'Star Day' is a Ticker tradition... begun for just one special person. Now... I give it to the world. If you want to know the story, you'll find it scattered across the Vallis. Today is too happy a day to tell it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0050Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Two Ostrons are freed during a daring operation, marry, have a child. I'm sure you'll be pleased to know that young 'Tenno' Helnung is doing quite well and taking after their mother on the scaffolds." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0060Ticker_en.ogg)
- "It's trite, but it has to be said: be brave with that heart of yours, Stardust. At the end of the day, those we love are what matters. Don't leave things unsaid." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0070Ticker_en.ogg)
- "The only family that extraordinary creatures such as we are permitted is the family whom we choose - and a fine family that is. Here's to us, Stardust." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0080Ticker_en.ogg)
- "You raised yourself. You'll have questions, and nobody will be there to answer them. I want you to know: I'm here, Stardust. I won't always have the answer, but I will listen, and what I tell you will be true." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0090Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Spotted Biz and Little Duck boarding her rail tractor, bound for parts unknown. I guess they celebrate today in their own way. Lots of history those two. They'll work it out."' [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0100Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Nobody's as hard on Eudico as Eudico. She's made mistakes, and her demons are loud. I try to dazzle 'em with love. Millions are alive today because there's a light in there that people believe in. We don't often see things as they are, so much as we are." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0110Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Everybody's hurting, Stardust. Some of them hurt you. That's a given. Secret to a happy life? Find the ones worth hurting for. Happy Star Day." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0120Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Star Days celebrates life and love and the value of those closest to us. Heaven may have nicer weather, but you can't beat Hell for the company. Here's to you, Stardust." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0130Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Love isn't just for lovers, Stardust. It's for friends, family, your bestest Kavat. Love is an act of forgiveness, a tender look, a silent understanding. Today is about all of us and knowing that we have more binding us together than. Pushing. Us. Apart." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0140Ticker_en.ogg)
- "I was never much one for looks or physique. I longed for those who sang songs only I could hear. Falling into the eyes of someone like that, a girl feels like she's getting away with something." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0150Ticker_en.ogg)
- "I have loved, and I have lost. 'Their absence has gone through me like thread through a needle,' as someone once said. All that I do is stitched with their color." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0160Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Words and deeds are seeds, Stardust. They may seem like nothing in the moment, but they can fall from you and begin growing in others. Fortuna used to be barren, is what I'm saying. You've given us an orchard. Happy Star Day." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0170Ticker_en.ogg)

### Varzia/Quotes

[全セリフ](varzia.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Varzia/Quotes)

**Ambient**

- "We're on the verge of war. Take your sweetness where you can." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0240Povia_en.ogg)
- "Ha, look at you, looking so fine. No wonder your uncle Teshin's proud." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0250Povia_en.ogg)
- "Inventory, secrets and scars. No need to put them all on display." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0260Povia_en.ogg)
- "Aya, regular and Regal, bring it here. Ayatans to the sweet thing over there." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0270Povia_en.ogg)
- "The kicker isn't that you kids brought down an Empire. It's that half of you don't remember doing it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0280Povia_en.ogg)
- "Rumours were true. The vault of history is open." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0290Povia_en.ogg)
- "After that Sentient scum nearly killed me, I swore I'd never take anything for granted. Not a breath, not a wink... not a kiss." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0300Povia_en.ogg)
- "Tempting Old War treasures on display." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0310Povia_en.ogg)
- "Keep it moving!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0320Povia_en.ogg)
- "It's fine if all you want is a training warframe, instead of a Prime. We all start somewhere." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0330Povia_en.ogg)
- "Whatever Tenno and Dax may have been to one another in the past, that's in the past." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVBarks0340Povia_en.ogg)

**Idle**

- "Don't sweat the Dax thing. My oaths died with the Golden Lords. Looking this good in uniform, oh is just a bonus." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0050Povia_en.ogg)
- "Teshin. I dunno if I wanna slam him against a wall or have him slam me against one." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0060Povia_en.ogg)
- "Time was I'd have had a thief like Maroo up on multiple charges. Won't be slapping cuffs on her now. Unless she wants me to, of course." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0070Povia_en.ogg)
- "Teshin's chapter-and-verse, until he gets his weapon in his hand... I never got tired of watching that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0080Povia_en.ogg)
- "You could live a thousand years and never wake to a face like hers. I slept a thousand and look what I got. What do you think? A career con like her and an old Dax like me?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0090Povia_en.ogg)
- "'S weird. I barely remember the faces of the Seven. But that black-sash Yaskutai who served our mess table? Ha, every little bead of her sweat stands out like a diamond in my mind..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0100Povia_en.ogg)
- "Teshin's like his blade, if you ask me. Cold, hard, and in desperate need of a good grinding." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0110Povia_en.ogg)
- "We need to be ready for the sentients this time. I already got out of one coffin. So no stinking machine is going to put me in another." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0120Povia_en.ogg)
- "My time is past, but damn, I miss the heft of a weapon... the stench of super heated plasma. Uh, I'd be fighting alongside you if I didn't have this thieving little miscreant to watch over. You can bet on it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0130Povia_en.ogg)
- "Not looking to settle any old scores with your kind, Tenno. Working with Aya... I saw the way your amps carved people I loved in two. Don't think I didn't. But... we're all different people now." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0140Povia_en.ogg)
- "I wear the uniform because I believe in the principles we died for. But the day will come when I take if off and lay it to rest. Hahaha, and, if I'm too bloody-minded, hopefully some pretty thing is there to do it for me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0150Povia_en.ogg)
- "You know how it is to serve in silence. We SAW things. The Yuvan Theatre. The Naberus Revels. Not the kind of things you can... convey... to people who just weren't there. Am I right?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0160Povia_en.ogg)
- "Not sure I should let you handle these goods with all that Orokin blood on your hands. Kidding! I'm glad they're dead and I hope it hurt." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0170Povia_en.ogg)
- "I see there's been a whole lot of... valiant attempts to make Frames up to the Prime standard since my time. Bless them. They did the best they could with what they had." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0180Povia_en.ogg)
- "You're not going to settle for a rank-and-file Warframe, are ya? Not when you could have Vanguard class?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0190Povia_en.ogg)
- "The Orokin were inhumane scum. The absolute dregs. But sweet tender mercy, could they design. The Primes they created have never been matched." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0200Povia_en.ogg)
- "Primes were reserved for the Prime Vanguard. The best of the best, and the rest... are adequate, I guess. If you're satisfied with merely adequate." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0210Povia_en.ogg)
- "When your life's on the line and you're staring down that raging Eidolon, nothing will keep you in one piece better than a Prime. If you value your shiny ass, bring me that Aya." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0220Povia_en.ogg)
- "Some Warframes were Prime from the start. Others earned it. Ballas never told the whole truth." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMPVIdle0230Povia_en.ogg)

### Vena/Quotes

[全セリフ](vena.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Vena/Quotes)

**As Railjack Crew › Idle**

- "If this is just another pointless errand, I will stab you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0110Vena_en.ogg)
- "No, no. It's fine. Take your time." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0120Vena_en.ogg)
- "Did you know the human body has between 4.5 to 5.7 liters of blood in it?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0130Vena_en.ogg)
- "4.9. You are definitely a 4.9. What?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0140Vena_en.ogg)
- "You don't need the rest of the crew, do you? We can do this with just us." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0150Vena_en.ogg)
- "How do you manage to get all the blood out from between your floor tiles?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0160Vena_en.ogg)
- "They never quite prepare you for how much of your time in space is going to be spent standing around... waiting... to kill things." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0170Vena_en.ogg)
- "Blood in zero gravity makes the prettiest little corpuscles. Want to see?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0180Vena_en.ogg)
- "There are actually three universal rules: Death, taxes, and 'I will make my own fun'. Understand?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0190Vena_en.ogg)
- "If we don't do something interesting soon, I will crash this ship into an asteroid just to watch you scream." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRJIdle0200Vena_en.ogg)

### Vox Solaris (Quest)/Transcript

[全セリフ](vox-solaris-quest-transcript.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Vox_Solaris_(Quest)/Transcript)

**Random Fortuna Broadcasts**

- Nef Anyo's broadcast: "Are you in debt Solaris? Then you'll need to push yourself, work harder, longer. The Void does not give onto the freeloader." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRandNefTran0020NefAnyo_en.ogg)
- Nef Anyo's broadcast: "I was once like you, poor, indebted, lazy. Then the Void spoke to me, it said 'work harder' and so I did. I earned what I have and now Fortuna, it is your turn. Listen to the Void. Work. Harder." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRandNefTran0030NefAnyo_en.ogg)

### Cetus/Quotes

[全セリフ](cetus.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Cetus/Quotes)

**Female Ostrons**

- "The bailer if you would, Doh-ga. Ten ampules ready for the crucible." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0010OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Those are fine boots, but they won't last. You want leather, wooden-pegged. Her oil will rot that rubber within a week." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0020OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Swazdo-lah, surah. If you will, inspect the quality of this black skin. See how it comes away beneath the fingernail? How succulent would this be on your tongue? Succulent-utz." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0030OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Abyssal snail spawn! As served on the tables of Corpus royalty! One thousand Platinum per dropper, yes?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0040OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Toilet bags! Handy for those on the go! Bodily waste converted to odorless crystals. Easy to dispose! Also, I have jewelery available in two colors!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0050OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Swazdo-lah, surah." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0060OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Well met." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0070OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Be well." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0080OstFem_en.ogg)
- "You see and are seen!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0090OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Utz! You must be watchful of some offworlders." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0100OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Need to stay awake? Chew some vandal bark! We also have dried ponda milk, and a wide range of charpi! Legal in several jurisdictions, reasonable prices!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0120OstFem_en.ogg)
- "I have seen the inside of the temple, and she has taught me much. Know your future. One cut, and your path lays itself out! Your future laid bare in a drop of blood!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0130OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Harpu! Melted Vobi butter and freshly brewed Chimurr. Salt and balb flour extra! Harpu! Hot Harpu!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0140OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Swazdo-lah, surah." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0150OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Well met." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0160OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Be well." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0170OstFem_en.ogg)
- "You see and are seen!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0180OstFem_en.ogg)
- "Fresh fish to feed the family, with eyes or without! Black fish, gulper, mesopelagic, bathypelagic, from shallows and river." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronFemaleGen11.ogg)

**Female Child Ostrons**

- "You'll never find me! (laughs) Find me, find me, find me!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronFemaleCGen1.ogg)
- "Coming, mother!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronFemaleCGen2.ogg)
- "My father is no longer in that line of business, surah. You'll have to find another canoe paddler." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronFemaleCGen3.ogg)
- "I have a pet Kavat. She is black and white. Her name is Woonacht. It means 'day and night'. Do you have a pet?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronFemaleCGen4.ogg)
- "Leaving on the inside, tempting fate. Sitting on the outside, dead man wait! Don't go, don't go, the wives all cry. Ha-ha-ha, ho-ho-ho, the men wave goodbye! Well met, well met, the dead come home. Avanti, avanti, kingdom of bone! The gate goes clack, the sun rolls back. Nothing to be done for them, a graveyard plaque!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronFemaleCGen5.ogg)
- "(laughs)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronFemaleCGen6.ogg)

**Male Ostrons**

- "Eight casks, old man. Five fourty weights of flesh." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0010OstMale_en.ogg)
- "She gives, we live." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0020OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Be well." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0030OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Doh-ga! Doh-ga! Temple flesh, rendered this morning! A small quantity of Temple Kuva! Available for the right client! For fascia, for oils, for matters of integumentarial and subcutaneous. Available to all for the right price! Gold for blood, people! Gold for blood!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0040OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Powders and dyes! Newly arrived at great risk from the Eurasian zone! Display your love with a streak of vermilion. Highlight those eyes with a dash of rare cerulean! Powders and dyes!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0060OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Swazdo-lah, surah." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0070OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Well met." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0080OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Be well." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0090OstMale_en.ogg)
- "You see and are seen." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0100OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Be well." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0140OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Ah, traveler. This morning by her grace, the temple offered of herself a particularly rich find of cellular proto-essence just below the stratum germinativum." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0150OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Swazdo-lah! Lok heb! Look here! Wine! Imbued with the etheric signature of a person you wish to be rid of! Spouse? Enemy? Ah, you drink them in the evening, and expel them by morning, eh? Guaranteed results!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0160OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Doctor Tanak's cerebral nutrients! Available in Cetus for the first time! The product of more than ten year's study! The venerable doctor now brings his range of livers, ales, humors, tinctures and unguents to you!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0170OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Swazdo-lah, surah." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0180OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Be well." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0190OstMale_en.ogg)
- "You see and are seen." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGen0200OstMale_en.ogg)
- "Finest masks, created by third-generation maskers! Herald your line and protect your bellows from gilding! Pride and health for the cost of a single purchase!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleGen5.ogg)
- "The blanket piece is ready for hoisting, old man!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleGen11.ogg)
- "Dah-dup. Lift her gently now. Flensers make ready. Airing board ready to receive her fascia." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleGen12.ogg)
- "Hand off your scrubs to the retrievers. Know that you see and are seen." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleGen13.ogg)

**Male Child Ostrons**

- "Be superior to nothing but your former self. Be well, surah!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleCGen1.ogg)
- "Coming, father!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleCGen2.ogg)
- "Mother says I shouldn't hate them for what they did. She says that things only grow when watered. But I want to hate them." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleCGen3.ogg)
- "I can't stop to talk, mama needs Vobi butter before the Harpu congeals!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleCGen4.ogg)
- "Find me, find me, find me! You'll never find me! (laughs)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleCGen5.ogg)
- "My father was lost at sea, surah. I now live by the lander pads. Can you spare a coin or two?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleCGen6.ogg)
- "(laughs)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:OstronMaleCGen7.ogg)

### Deep Archimedea/Quotes

[全セリフ](deep-archimedea.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Deep_Archimedea/Quotes)

**Idle**

- **First time**
- Loid: "Ahem. Attention, Tenno! Now that Loid and the Cavia have settled in, I feel I can be of more use. The laboratories are for research, after all, and science is calling! Henceforth, I shall be dividing my time between the upper Necralisk and down here in the Sanctum Anatomica. Keep it on the hush-hush, if you don't mind?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAnnounce0010Loid_en.ogg)
- **Subsequent times**
- Loid: "Oh gosh. You appear to have caught me moonlighting. Mum's the word, eh?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0020Loid_en.ogg)
- Loid: "Yes... Your eyes aren't deceiving you. Old Necraloid is working in the Sanctum now. So many responsibilities to juggle." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0030Loid_en.ogg)
- Loid: "I wouldn't want Loid to struggle, so here I am. Such a pity when people are too proud to ask for help." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0040Loid_en.ogg)
- Loid: "Tenno... I need your help to help Loid. It's what Master Entrati would have wanted." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0050Loid_en.ogg)
- Loid: "I like it here. Lots of natural light for a basement. Odd that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0060Loid_en.ogg)
- Loid: "These Cavia seem like jolly fellows. Well. Most of them. Well. The bird, anyway." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0070Loid_en.ogg)
- Loid: "Word to the wise, Tenno. Do not ask about Bird 1 and 2. " [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0080Loid_en.ogg)

### Defection/Quotes

[全セリフ](defection.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Defection/Quotes)

**Flavor Dialogue**

- Stoic Defector: "Ratzan-narr, is there no rest?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0310Stoic_en.ogg)
- Stoic Defector: "Infestation called us here, no lie." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0320Stoic_en.ogg)
- Stoic Defector: "Better not be a trap Tenno." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0330Stoic_en.ogg)
- Stoic Defector: "This again? Fight or die? Rraahudd" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0340Stoic_en.ogg)
- Stoic Defector: "They said Tenno kill us for sport. Did they lie?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0350Stoic_en.ogg)
- Stoic Defector: "Tenno? Steel Meridian send you?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0360Stoic_en.ogg)
- Stoic Defector: "So, Tenno not monsters?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0370Stoic_en.ogg)
- PTSD Defector: "Too loud. Too loud." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0380PTSD_en.ogg)
- PTSD Defector: "We came to help, the distress call was a PRETAkkang trap." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0390PTSD_en.ogg)
- PTSD Defector: "Tenno get nightmares? I do." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0400PTSD_en.ogg)
- PTSD Defector: "Escape Grineer to die here? No!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0410PTSD_en.ogg)
- PTSD Defector: "Eyes closed, there is only blood." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0420PTSD_en.ogg)
- Pacifist Defector: "The Infestation can think. It can hunt." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0430Pacifist_en.ogg)
- Pacifist Defector: "Not the plan! Plan was no fighting." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0440Pacifist_en.ogg)
- Pacifist Defector: "This ship, our dream... both dead." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0450Pacifist_en.ogg)
- Pacifist Defector: "Look at me like that? You were made to kill too, Tenno." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0460Pacifist_en.ogg)
- Pacifist Defector: "Said no more fighting. Rahm here we are!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0470Pacifist_en.ogg)
- Pacifist Defector: "These sikkhat monsters feel no pain. Right?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0480Pacifist_en.ogg)
- Pacifist Defector: "How, Tenno? How you kill so much?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVFlavor0490Pacifist_en.ogg)
- Sargas Ruk: "Kavor wretches. Thought you were too good to be Grineer? But no Grineer would cower in this cesspool of genetic filth!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVRukFlavor0050SargasRuk_en.ogg)
- Sargas Ruk: "Pathetic. Wiggling like bait for the Infested. Let them rend your coward flesh a mercy compared to my judgment." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVRukFlavor0060SargasRuk_en.ogg)
- Sargas Ruk: "Did I not birth you? Feed you? Give you enemies to conquer? Ingrates." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVRukFlavor0070SargasRuk_en.ogg)
- Sargas Ruk: "You deserted to practice peace? I will bathe you in violence until you drown!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVRukFlavor0080SargasRuk_en.ogg)
- Sargas Ruk: "Delicate Kavor flowers say they saw too much? You have not seen anything yet!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVRukFlavor0090SargasRuk_en.ogg)
- Sargas Ruk: "Kavor with Tenno skoom? You betrayed your genes. You betrayed your Queens!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVRukFlavor0100SargasRuk_en.ogg)
- Sargas Ruk: "You were born of violence, you will die of violence! I command it!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVRukFlavor0110SargasRuk_en.ogg)
- Sargas Ruk: "You humiliate all Grineer! There is no higher crime." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVRukFlavor0120SargasRuk_en.ogg)
- Sargas Ruk: "Tenno, you aid and abed these fugitives? You too will face judgment." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DEVRukFlavor0130SargasRuk_en.ogg)

### Defense/Quotes

[全セリフ](defense.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Defense/Quotes)

**Stage Defense › Mid-Wave Banter (First Round Only)**

- Viktor: "Flare, are you trying to become a martyr?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0230Viktor_en.ogg)
- Flare: "Martyr? You think you can kill me?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0240Flare_en.ogg)
- Viktor: "You want to help Höllvania? When you couldn't even help your bandmates?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0250Viktor_en.ogg)
- Flare: "Trying to get under my skin? You of all people should know, my skin is much thicker than it used to be." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0260Flare_en.ogg)
- Viktor: "The fugitive Flare has brought filth to Höllvania. Filthy ideas. Filthy music. Filthy Techrot." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0310Viktor_en.ogg)
- Flare: "You said you were a fan. It's not our fault you didn't listen to our lyrics." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0320Flare_en.ogg)
- Lizzie: "Little Viktor whimpers. We made him the fool." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0290Lizzie_en.ogg)
- Flare: "It was all in good fun. Until it wasn't." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0300Flare_en.ogg)
- Lizzie: "Little Viktor wants to play." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0330Lizzie_en.ogg)
- Flare: "I already told him, we don't have any room in this band for hack wannabes." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0340Flare_en.ogg)
- Flare: "What's so dangerous about a little rock & roll, Viktor?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0270Flare_en.ogg)
- Lizzie: "Does he fear the corrupted youth will rise?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0280Lizzie_en.ogg)
- Flare: "We make music. He's killing the city. Tell me again who the real monster is?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0350Flare_en.ogg)
- Lizzie: "Demon protects, Viktor devours." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0360Lizzie_en.ogg)
- Flare: "A gracious shout out to my lovely friends providing security today." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0370Flare_en.ogg)
- Lizzie: "They're killing it!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0380Lizzie_en.ogg)
- Flare: "Höllvania! I need you to know: You are loved. You are needed. You are not disposable." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0390Flare_en.ogg)
- Lizzie: "Reject The Smiling One!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DDefExchUpper0400Lizzie_en.ogg)

### Duviri/Quotes/Citizens

[全セリフ](duviri-citizens.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Duviri/Quotes/Citizens)

**（ページ冒頭）**

- Quotes from Duviri's inhabitants.

**Unnamed Citizens › Sweeper (M)**

- "Don't these idiots know the Dax might inspect us at any moment?"
- "(whistling)"
- "Fix this, fix that, sweep this, sweep that..."
- "Yes yes what do you want?"
- "I'm busy, make it quick."
- "What? What do you want now?"
- "Ah, wonderful."
- "Ah, to what do I owe the honor?"
- "Out with it or move on."
- "Sling your hook, I have work to do."

**Unnamed Citizens › Sweeper (F)**

- "Well if I'm devoured today, my children will inherit a clean house."
- "Work, like life, it never ends... it is merely... endured."
- "Oh my achin' back."
- "What? What is it?"
- "Go on. Move along. No lollygagging around here."
- "Do I know you?"
- "Oh, good day my liege. The airs on this one."
- "They come, they go, they come, they go."
- "Oh. Lovely. Another one. Yes? What?"
- "Ugh, missed a spot."
- "A woman feels like she's swept the same floor for as long as she can remember..."

**Unnamed Citizens › Sedentary (M)**

- "Torture would be better than this. But then I guess this is torture."
- "There goes that Drifter again."
- "Void-damned kids! Disrespecting their seniors!"
- "First day of the rest of my life. Again. Wa-hoo."
- "Well, at least this place will fall into the Void one day."
- "My toe's itching! Oh no, wait. It stopped."
- "Bring on the big wyrm. Get it over with."
- "I don't know why I thought today would be any different."
- "Same old, same old."
- "Well, it would be nice to want something once in a while."

**Unnamed Citizens › Sedentary (F)**

- "Eternity. Well, I can't say I like it."
- "Should I break something? Scream a little? No. I don't think I'll bother."
- "Wonder what it's like to be a tree. Or a rock. Or a fish."
- "Yesterday, my mood was "bored". Today, it is also "bored". Who knows what tomorrow will bring?"
- "Guess I could go get run over by a Kaithe again."
- "Eighteen million bottles of Kuva on the wall..."
- "No. I can't quite believe it. What kind of benevolent creator would consign me to this?"
- "I'm going to take off this mask! No. I'm not."
- "It's good that I don't remember. It's good."
- "I look into the Void, the Void looks into me looking into the Void. "

**Unnamed Citizens › Brisk (M)**

- "Let's not waste the day His Majesty gave us!"
- "Is that... you know, I think it is!"
- "Whatever you may say about Duviri, it's never the same day twice."
- "I've been doing this all my life and I'm not even tired!"
- "Who, I ask you, who would ever want to live anywhere else?"
- "Stay away from the pools and ponds! The Golden Maws are anything but companionable. They're ravenous!"
- "The trick is keeping busy. Saves you from having to do any thinking."
- "Thrax is on his throne, and all is right with the world."
- "Oh, people love to complain! Know what I say? The King is doing his best. Besides, I'd like to see you do better."
- "Oh it doesn't matter to me one little bit what color the sky turns. I think this place is beautiful."
- "Prosper the day, stranger!"

**Unnamed Citizens › Brisk (F)**

- "It's okay! It's going to be fine! It always is!"
- "Go stand under a Materlith and then tell me this isn't a world full of love."
- "Oh! For a moment there I thought that you were the Drifter! Like my little life would ever get that exciting!"
- "Well hello there!"
- "What a beautiful day! If you want to see the world don't stand in one place! Go go go!"
- "Feels good to get a body moving."
- "Hey friend!"
- "What's the good word?"
- "Nice to see you! Any news?"
- "Always nice to see a friendly face. What brings you here?"

**Unnamed Citizens › Shopkeeper**

- "You wouldn't be interested in the junk I have for sale. This clutter is for common Duviri folk, not heroes."
- "I'd swear this place used to be busier. Couldn't remember when, though, if you asked me."
- "Alas, my trade is in horseshoe nails and boot laces. Not mighty guns or shining swords."
- "Hey. What brings you around?"
- "My shoddy wares are far too humble for the likes of you, good Drifter."
- "Still strugglin' against the King, eh? Good luck to you. Me? I know my place."
- "Lookin' real dangerous today, friend."
- "I mean, yeah, some do say Thrax should be murdered every day for a million years... but everyone agrees, he gave us some nice buildings."
- "I've been in this business a long time, met a lot of people and I can tell you this: you're not who you think you are. There's a lot more to you than that."

**Unnamed Citizens › Cave Dweller**

- "When one falls in love with what cannot see, one falls in love with magic."
- "You may as well laugh. Anxiety changes nothing."
- "It is said you cannot cheat an honest man. Untrue. To cheat an honest man one does not appeal to their greed, but to their pity."
- "One cannot repair people, only love them. Let go of the fallacy, or be dragged behind it."
- "If it is stupid and it works, it is not stupid."
- "To adjust perspective on suffering remember that the shape of both comedy and horror are identical."
- "Life is both unkind and unpredictable. But it would be worse if it were unkind and predictable."
- "Distance yourself from hurry, from chatter. Sit in silence. Sit in silence long enough that your mind, out of need, converses with itself. From this will be learned a great many things. Things astounding, things terrible, all useful, all impossible to disregard. In this we survive both life, and death."
- "Emperors, kings, popes and tyrants. All tell us things. Strange things. Conflicting things. Confusing things. They tell us these things not so we believe. They tell us these things so we believe nothing. And in believing nothing, do nothing."
- "A nurse held hand after hand day after day as soul after soul fled body after body. She believed her mind had fled and her soul would be next. No. Our minds save us. Saved her. Her mind ferried away horrors before returning for the next day's passengers. The things she believed lost were only borrowed, as company for her savior's journey. This, this is good to remember."
- "You have a shadow. It is that which you wish you were not, but are."
- "If one wishes to be forever young, one must be forever learning. "
- "Suffering and misery do not have to be one and the same. Men and women will suffer for anything, so long as they believe it to be meaningful. When their labor is without meaning... then suffering truly becomes suffering."
- "A mother to a misbehaving child once said "Do it until your father laughs." I say to you, my eyes to Heaven: "Do it until your father laughs.""
- "You are trapped by your ability to symbolize. Do you understand?"

**Unnamed Citizens › Boatman**

- "You can't put a price on a view like that."
- "My pa always said: there's two ways to be rich. Get a lot or want little. This big sky? All I've ever needed."
- "Headed somewhere?"
- "The snap of her sails and the creak of her hull are music to my ears."
- "Wherever a body needs to be, my girl and I can take them there."
- "Mind the crosswinds over Lonesome Outlook and we'll keep her true."
- "Wind's picking up."
- "Another day, girl. Another blessed day."
- "The world down here may be upside down, but up there... up there things are always as they were meant to be."
- "Well. Best get my boots on. Will and wind wait for none."

**Unnamed Citizens › The Lovers**

- "(sigh of relief)"
- "(laughter)"
- "Should we? I think we should."
- "In a little bit. Let's just enjoy the light."
- "It'll be fine. You'll see."
- "It will always be like this. I promise."
- "I should have been there. I hope she knew."
- "What do I think? I think you know what I think."
- "It will happen. There's always time."
- "And that was that. Extraordinary."

**Unnamed Citizens › The Reader**

- "I find something new in this each time I read it."
- "Delightful."
- "Fascinating. Absolutely fascinating."
- "No artist tolerates reality. And yes that was something I read in a book."
- "I'm almost done. Give me another ten minutes."

**Unnamed Citizens › The Friend**

- "There's also a world out here, you realize."
- "(humming)"
- "You should read to me sometime. I'd like that."
- "If you don't put that down we're going to be late."
- "I watched an Orowyrm chase a Kaithe and you completely missed it."

**Children › Anger**

- "We're gonna take him Shake him, break him, Roast him, toast him Bind him, blind him!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesAnger00280ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Punch him, crunch him Snatch him, scratch him, Flay him, slay him Good and DEAD!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesAnger00290ChildF_en.ogg)
- "If only fire melted stone Oh how people's wrath would burn." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesAnger00300ChildM_en.ogg)
- "If angry words could break a bone Oh how the world would moan and groan." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesAnger00310ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Let your blood boil Let your teeth grind, Let your fire rise 'Cause it's Lodun's Day!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesAnger00320ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Better make a fist Better start a fray, Knock a fella down 'Cause it's Lodun's Day." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesAnger00330ChildM_en.ogg)

**Children › Envy**

- "Something's watching through the window, Watching you and watching me... Wants what we have, wants to BE us! We know that can never be." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesEnvy00340ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Something's waiting, getting closer, Watching, waiting for its day, Something needy, cruel and greedy... Keep that hungry thing at bay!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesEnvy00350ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Oh Mister Bombastine We know why you act so mean, Everyone else has more than you Everyone else is bored of you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesEnvy00360ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Oh Mister Bombastine We know why your eyes are green, Someone else had a sweeter voice Someone else was the King's new choice." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesEnvy00370ChildF_en.ogg)

**Children › Fear**

- "Go hide your face, In a lonesome place, Many miles away from here." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00130ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Where the night winds wail, Till your face turns pale, And the stones all mock your fear!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00140ChildF_en.ogg)
- "What's that whisper on the breeze?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00150ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Just the rustling in the trees." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00160ChildF_en.ogg)
- "What's that tread upon the stair?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00170ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Nothing, darling, nothing there." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00180ChildF_en.ogg)
- "What's that rattling at my door?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00190ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Just the wind and nothing more." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00200ChildF_en.ogg)
- "What's that breathing in my ear?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00210ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Nothing, darling, nothing here." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00220ChildF_en.ogg)
- "What's that gleaming sharp and bright?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00230ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Hold your tongue and say GOODNIGHT!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00240ChildF_en.ogg)
- "What are you afraid of? Empty eyes and, Distant cries and Wolves who walk in human guise?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00250ChildF_en.ogg)
- "What are you afraid of? Creaks and thuds and, Trickling blood and Mouths and noses filled with mud?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00260ChildM_en.ogg)
- "What are you afraid of? Nails in your knees and, Faces in the trees and Clowns with hands all full of bees?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesFear00270ChildF_en.ogg)

**Children › Joy**

- "Grin, grin, grin!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesHappy00070ChildM_en.ogg)
- "If smiles were all it took, We would all be happy With just one look!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesHappy00080ChildF_en.ogg)
- "There's a face in the sky, With a great big smile, So we smile right back today." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesHappy00090ChildM_en.ogg)
- "There's a man in a wall, With a grin like a skull, So we grin right back today." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesHappy00100ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Even when your house is burning down Even when a fool has stolen your crown, You can smile instead You can smile!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesHappy00110ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Even when you can't afford your tax Even when your husband's been killed by Dax, You can smile instead You can smile!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesHappy00120ChildF_en.ogg)

**Children › Sorrow**

- "Lady, lady, why do you weep, Tear at your hair And cry in your sleep?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesSad00010ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Grief and woe have made me a thing... Fit but to sing At the will of the King." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesSad00020ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Oh the wall rose high... And it wished it could cry With the tears of a thousand eyes." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesSad00030ChildM_en.ogg)
- "For Sorrow it sought... But it never ever thought How it already kept its prize." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesSad00040ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Cry for a dead love Cry for your shame, Cry for the loss Of your own good name." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesSad00050ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Cry for the day and Cry for the hour, Cry for the lady Locked in the tower." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesSad00060ChildF_en.ogg)

**Children › Kullervo's Hold**

- "Kullervo, Kullervo, warrior-born, From his mother's breast he was torn. Doomed to go wandering all forlorn, Kullervo, Kullervo, made to mourn." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0010ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Kullervo, Kullervo, swaddled in lies, Raised to a life of groans and cries, Took to his task with sorrowing sighs, Kullervo, Kullervo, death was your prize." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0020ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Kullervo, Kullervo, sworn to the sword, Sweated and slaved for the head of the horde, Bruised and bludgeoned and scarred and gored, Kullervo, Kullervo, warrior's ward." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0030ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Kullervo, Kullervo, far he strayed. Lips to the steel he blessed and prayed, Whispered promises that boy made! Kullervo, Kullervo, friend to the blade." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0040ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Kullervo, Kullervo, ragged and thin, Tracked back the path so steeped in sin, All the way back where it did begin, Kullervo, Kullervo, sought for his kin." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0050ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Kullervo, Kullervo, burdens he bore, Many the woes and horrors he saw! Tyrants and conquerors ravaging the poor, Kullervo, Kullervo, witness to war." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0060ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Kullervo, Kullervo, stranger to mirth, Spoke with the ragged who toiled in the earth, Learned of the truth of the days of his birth, Kullervo, Kullervo, what was he worth?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0070ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Kullervo, Kullervo, stood all alone Over the graves of the ones he'd known. Homestead warmth turned to ashes and bone, Kullervo, Kullervo, cold as a stone." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0080ChildF_en.ogg)
- "Kullervo, Kullervo, broke like a flood, Ripped and he ravaged with a slash-slice-thud! Many lay headless, armless, in the mud, Kullervo, Kullervo, bathed in blood." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0090ChildM_en.ogg)
- "Kullervo, Kullervo, long did he weep, In his own breast his sword drove deep. Down to the dark and a merciful sleep, Kullervo, Kullervo, your secrets we keep." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRymesKull0100ChildF_en.ogg)

**Children › Humming**

- "(humming)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesHum00380ChildM_en.ogg)
