- "(humming)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesHum00420ChildF_en.ogg)
- "(hums the melody of The Duviri Paradox)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DChildRhymesHum00430ChildF_en.ogg)

**Named Characters › Pip**

- Pip: "To the Void with you, Fergin! The King says I can play as loud as I like! What I wouldn't give to hear his grumpy old voice again." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11400Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: "You ever gonna make that move? He's never coming back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11410Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: ""You ever do requests? You do? Go to the edge and jump!" That's what he'd say. Every day." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11420Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: ""Komi is for true Orokin! Shawzin's nothing but a Dax toy!" Ah, he was full of himself." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11430Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: "You know what he'd say to me? "You think you're making Father proud? Playing Shawzin all day?" Like I was nothing but a loafer." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11440Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: "Real exciting game you got going on over there! He was there every day. Then one day, he just wasn't." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11450Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: "What was that, Fergin? Can't hear you over the crowds! I keep thinking I'm gonna look up and see him scowling across at me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11460Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: "I've got an audience! I don't see anyone lining up to play Komi with you! Every day it feels like a little bit more of this place is lost." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11470Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: "Oh really? Why don't you come over here and say that to my FACE? I know just what he'd say to that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11480Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: "This one's for you, Fergin! He hated my music. Hated me, too. Void, I miss him." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11490Pip_en.ogg)
- Pip: ""Shuddup," he would say. "Who taught you to play, a kaithe's arse?" Now all I hear is this shawzin." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodPip11500Pip_en.ogg)

**Named Characters › Koral**

- (Note: This only includes Koral's dialogue when found at Mathila's Farm.)
- Koral: "I do not care so much for the denizens of the downbelow. They are ever so gruff."
- Koral: "We fear not, little friends, for though the world may shake and fume the end of the world has not quite come."
- Koral: "The world is ever such a big place. How shall someone as small as myself make their way in it? Such is the nature of adventure!"
- Koral: "When the weather grows warm, and mother is inattentive, I love to dip my head in the cool, bubbling stream. Mother shies me away from water, ever since father was taken by the sea. Or so she tells me."
- Koral: "I feel I owe the sweet, little citizens of the world a great debt of compassion. When I am heartsick they afford a great and abiding comfort."
- Koral: "Blessings upon you as broad as your compassionate shoulders, Outlaw."
- Koral: "I wonder what father would have had to say about you, Outlaw. Mother looks upon you kindly… and father was always contrary… so perhaps it is best you never did meet. Yes."
- Koral: "I find it curious - do you not? - that one has no memory of what they were before they were themself. As I remember nothing about it, I know nothing of it. And yet, I am certain, I was not always Koral. Were you always an Outlaw, I wonder?"
- Koral: "I think I should very much like to be a Tamm. I would live upon cress and milk and gambol with my friends and never ever be sad."
- Koral: "I wished to gather some water bugs for our supper. I asked the fishes if they had seen any, but they were greedy and wanted them for themselves and so said nothing."

**The Courtiers › Bombastine**

- Bombastine: "I suppose she is still chirping away on her perch of honor? Of course she is." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10890Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "My, don't you look fancy. Where do you get those outfits?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10900Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "If I could just borrow their powers. Lodun's fire. Sythel's lightning. Then I'd be a Wyrm to reckon with." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10910Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "The shoe will be on the other foot one day. Oh, yes." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10920Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "Days of being executed all behind you, are they? Be nice if you remembered your old friends." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10930Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "Look at you. Feared and respected wherever you go. Must be nice." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10940Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "Oh, to be a Drifter! No ties. No responsibilities. Nobody to take care of." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10950Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "Heard the King put a bounty on your head. How's it feel? To know you're worth that much to someone?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10960Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "Helping one of the others today, are you? It's okay. I understand. Not a problem." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10970Bombastine_en.ogg)
- Bombastine: "If I had your resources, I'd be on the throne by now. Bombastine the First. Just saying." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodBombastine10980Bombastine_en.ogg)

**The Courtiers › Lodun**

- Lodun: "What are you gawping at?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun10990Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "Peasants. Getting their filth all over everything." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11000Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "One day, Imp. One day I get to hear your scrawny neck go snap." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11010Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "I haven't forgotten how you spoke to me, "Drifter". I should have your tongue for that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11020Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "Oh, not AGAIN! THAT REALLY BOILS MY… URRRGH!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11030Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "MY TROOPS SHOULD HAVE BEEN HERE AN HOUR AGO!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11040Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "WHY ARE YOU EVEN RIDING A BLOODY KAITHE IF YOU DON'T KNOW HOW TO CONTROL IT?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11050Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "Someone find me some troops with some COMMON SODDING SENSE!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11060Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "Next time I'll stay in control. Keep out of Wyrm form. Dethrone the little bastard." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11070Lodun_en.ogg)
- Lodun: "One more second of that miserable bloody singing and I'll go to the edge and jump off." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLodun11080Lodun_en.ogg)

**The Courtiers › Luscinia**

- Luscinia: "Again and again I lose myself, and devastation follows. " [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11090Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "Did you come to mock me?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11100Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "If you dare come to me with pity, I shall throw it back in your face." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11110Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "Do you see the marks his anger left on the land?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11120Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "I weep for ownerless toys, and empty chairs. Letters unsent and love long forgotten. Should I weep for you, too?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11130Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "Bombastine envies me. The King's Favorite. He wishes we could trade places. So do I." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11140Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "If the Void truly preserves consciousness… then there is no hope of an end!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11150Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "I was created to be Sorrow. Written into being. To serve as a lesson. Can that change?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11160Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "I have known happiness. Even love. I shall not speak of it. I shall only sing." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11170Luscinia_en.ogg)
- Luscinia: "The fish never complains of the salt of the sea, for it is all he has ever known. But I? I cannot forget what once was." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodLuscinia11180Luscinia_en.ogg)

**The Courtiers › Mathila**

- Mathila: "We've all experienced unpleasantness, but that's no reason to dwell upon it! Chin up!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11190Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "Nothing puts one's woes in perspective like helping people who REALLY need it! People with REAL worries!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11200Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "People need to understand that unhappiness is a choice! A little exercise sees me right as rain!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11210Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "People complain about Thrax doing this horrible thing, or that horrible thing. But what about all the good? It's never boring! Have they stopped to think of that?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11220Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "I know, I know, the Dax are bullies and the weather is apocalyptic. But at least no one's died! Today." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11230Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "Yes yes I know being persecuted can be difficult, but try living with someone who won't stop going on about it. Positive thinking! That's the way!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11240Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "A smile is like a yawn. Someone does it and pretty soon you're doing it yourself." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11250Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "If you're at a loose end, you can always help Koral take care of her animal friends." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11260Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "I always swear I will not indulge in that horrid… wyrm behavior… But I just get so. Very. Very. Very. Happy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11270Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "People are so keen to write me off. As if I don't have the hardest job of any of us." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11280Mathila_en.ogg)
- Mathila: "You certainly make old Teshin happy. Perhaps one day he'll even tell you that himself." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodMathila11290Mathila_en.ogg)

**The Courtiers › Sythel**

- Sythel: "We're both more dangerous than we look you and I. Keep your distance. " [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11300Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Were you followed?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11310Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Keep your hands where I can see them." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11320Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "You don't know me. You have no idea what I'm capable of!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11330Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Beetles in the earth. Biting oh-so-gently. Tunneling. Up through my skin." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11340Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "I've seen them. The black-eyed children. Pointing at me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11350Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "The first scholar looked into the Void and he feared it. And his fear took shape. Do you understand? That's how all this started!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11360Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "I can't stay in here forever. I'll starve. Get bitten. Catch the swarming rot. Little eggs under my eyelids!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11370Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Listen! There. Did you hear it? Rap. Tap. Tap." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11380Sythel_en.ogg)
- Sythel: "Not safe here. They'll find me. Her with her carriage. Always nosing around. Got to move. Find a new place." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGenBarksNoMoodSythel11390Sythel_en.ogg)

### Follie's Hunt/Quotes

[全セリフ](follie-s-hunt.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Follie's_Hunt/Quotes)

**Lobby › Idle**

- Zorba: "You need to enter the painting. There's no going back now, Tenno." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLobbyLoiterOngoing0330AspirantZorba_en.ogg)
- Zorba: "The painting is Truth. Face it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLobbyLoiterOngoing0340AspirantZorba_en.ogg)
- Zorba: "How did Follie's painting ever survive?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLobbyLoiterOngoing0350AspirantZorba_en.ogg)
- Zorba: "The Arbiters of Hexis will never forgive Vay Hek for his desecration of Vesper Relay." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLobbyLoiterOngoing0360AspirantZorba_en.ogg)
- Zorba: "Vesper Relay took a direct hit from a Fomorian. How did that Shadowgraph even survive?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLobbyLoiterOngoing0370AspirantZorba_en.ogg)

**Random Background Announcements**

- ???: "Make sure you paint within the frame. We don't want to be messy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0010Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Relax. Paint what you feel." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0020Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "The brush is a part of you. Don't fight it. Let it guide you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0030Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "There! No need to rush. Let it out, a little at a time." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0040Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Do your feelings have a shape? Can you paint them a face?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0050Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Drip, drip, drip. That's right. Let the paint do the bleeding." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0060Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Just let your brush do what it wants. It might surprise you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0070Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Round and round and up and down!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0080Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Breathe all of the bad into a big beautiful balloon and... let it go." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0090Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "It won't do you any good to get cross." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0100Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "If you don't fight me, then the paint won't fight you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0110Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "The paint doesn't care what's real or unreal, you can make it into whatever you need!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0120Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Whatever's scaring you, you can turn it into a picture! Then it won't ever be able to get out again. Will it?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0130Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Breathe in, breathe out. Let the paint flow." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0140Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "If we need to scream, we don't do with our mouths. We do it with our pictures!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0150Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Fold it over and press, and look! Could that be a pretty Lepidrian, or perhaps a happy Thasmite?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0160Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Sometimes you don't know what it's going to be until you've finished." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0170Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Brushes are not for chewing! Paint is not for drinking!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0180Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "No throwing! No flicking!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0190Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "The smooth white page is listening. Can the paint tell it a story?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0200Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "What do you see? Can you show me? Can you... paint it for me?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0210Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "There are no wrong answers, I want to see what you see. Just pick up the brush." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0220Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Do you feel happy? There are tears in your eyes. Are they happy tears?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0230Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "You just need to paint. That's all. Everything will be made clear, in time." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0240Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Remember, the painting isn't real. Nothing can hurt you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0250Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "It's just balloons. Nothing. To be scared of." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0260Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "You're seeing all of your bad qualities - hate, anger, distrust. You are strangling them." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0270Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "You say you're seeing faces? Whose faces? How many? How many of them have you killed?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0280Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "Cast the bad thoughts out of your mind and onto the canvas. Out they go! Splish, splash, splosh!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0290Archimedean_en.ogg)
- ???: "There's no sense in hiding anything away! Secrets will just fester in your skull." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBackgroundAnnouncements0300Archimedean_en.ogg)

### Fortuna/Quotes

[全セリフ](fortuna.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Fortuna/Quotes)

**（ページ冒頭）**

- Idle dialogue by NPCs in Fortuna. See Solaris Language for explanations of slang used.

**Unknown Radio Dispatcher › Female Solaris**

- "Appreciate a meetway and assist. Got clawed pretty bad. In need of go-go juice and action docs. Holler back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRadio1480SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Any outbound convoys got room for a take along? Come back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRadio1490SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Full load of Morphics and Rubedo. Eyeballing throwers and go-go juice for quick back-stroke. Any agents on the line?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRadio1500SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Four-two. Throwers unloaded me rig. Now deadheading toward the ammo lane." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRadio1180SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Bundled out on bird dog and a buyer. Any agents out there with a lead? Come back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRadio1190SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Backstroking convoy. Deadheaded and eyeballing silver stamp for honest work." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRadio1200SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)

**Unknown Radio Dispatcher › Male Solaris**

- "Negatory on the organ donor. Just copy-cat advertising chasing a death wish." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRadio0600SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Any one hailing from the Saturn convoy got their ears on? Come back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRadio0610SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Being redirected to a turtle racing corridor 2-4-Zeta." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRadio0620SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Agent flooring it to the rail. Eyes on." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRadio0640SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Wait from Venus' to southern platform's crater-faced. Swing wide." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRadio0900SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Dropped a furball of copy-cats right on Venus. Left them poppin' zits and hauled tail." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRadio0910SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Fatboy bundled out with merk-merks and bookin' it for the rail. Sure would appreciate in little cover on the back door. Come back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRadio0920SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Got Neptune in my back pocket and bound for my home 20." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRadio0930SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Pulling to in a loop. Having sort of trouble and got my nightgown on." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRadio0940SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)

**A › Female Solaris**

- **Directly right of the spawn point**
- "Look, you can breathe coolant for twenty years, or come with me! Merk-merks need backup! Six month run popping zits near Mercury. Come back flush! Easy money, old mate, easy!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1060SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "I'd scrub me wetware before they shelve me and no mistake. Scrub a few of them too, right out." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1070SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "I don't give a fig they're Corpus endorsed, one more witness for the Order of Profit comes around my hab early in the cycle, they gonna find me waiting with a shotgun, ready to void some muckin' warranties!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1370SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Wanna know how you die, kid? One day at a time, that's how." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1380SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Look, he's rarely there for you on a good day, sure. But he's always there for you on a bad one." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1390SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Me boyfriend break up with me yesterday. (chuckling) He's smart, he's handsome, his optical receptors have been malfunctioning for months. He told me: "I'm seeing someone else." I said: "(scoffs) I'm over HERE!" (laughing)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1400SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "You wanna wish for heaven? Go right ahead. But any history book will tell ya, what waits at the end of that dream... every time... is straight up genocide." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRebel1130SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Solaris United, uppity sorts who think they know how things should be! That's why they can't see things how they are. Things ARE as they should BE." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRebel1140SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- **Left of the spawn point, another one to the left of Eudico**
- "(Humming "We All Lift Together")"

**A › Male Solaris**

- **North of the spawn point, at the NPC you can watch the "We All Lift Together" cutscene**
- "I'm Solaris and proud. As are all me clan. The repos, the wasters. Those adrift in the bio-ways. Disgusting, no pride to any of them. I'll work hard. Me old man worked hard an' his old man 'efore him. No time for those who would rather rot in the streets. Me? None." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0320SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Ach. We work hard now, and for sure we suffer. We'll all be forgotten in the coming prosperity. Nef Anyo watches over us. The Order of the Profit too. Life is profit, ain't what they say?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0330SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Self-employed, me rear end. Only thing I own is second-stage toxic shock, the water I'm treading, and head full of bad dreams." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0340SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Checked into the machine shop to get me mic serviced. Can't bloody sleep 'cause I can't turn the bloody things off." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0350SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Gonna find me a recess and close me shutters for five. I'd mainline caff to get through this shift, but they upgraded the canisters and me gastro-compatibility upgrade ain't due till next week." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0360SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "So, the Corpus get here, right? Place is a firestorm. They turn the old Orokin terraforming back on and presto. Within a week we got plants and animals ain't nobody ever seen. I'm tellin' ya, either the tech is hiding something, or the planet is." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0370SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Butch of kids makin' a clubhouse in the ducts. Gotta call security. That racket they call music is a needle in me receptors." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0380SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "(Humming)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0382SUMaleOne_en.ogg)

**B › Female Solaris**

- **Right of Rude Zuud**
- "Me and an old mate traded shifts. I did his, he piked on mine. Repo? Hah! That dip-faced mucker won't have any parts left to repo, I find him." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker0990SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Wait. Wait, wait, wait, wait, wait. Slight the breaks there, Champ. You telling me there's something under Venus?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1000SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Quills, you see 'em everywhere these days if you know where to look. Corner of your eye, pulling strings." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1010SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "The Order said I could consolidate me debt by refinancin' me rig, using it as ...equity? I think? But now they say I owe more 'en ever, so I got myself extra shifts. Gonna dig me self out the old fashioned way, like me ma almost did." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1020SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Me old man got brain-shelved when I were a lad. Didn't make the payments. His brain-case is still there, locked up by the Taxmen, waiting for us to pay off his old rig. Never 'appening me lifetime of course, but me sprogs might get to meet their granddad someday. When they's grandfolk themselves." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1030SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Tim rig(?) failed. Canal 4 near went gaseous. Imagine that blowing through the habways, not everyone's a full conversion. What airborne Orokin coolant does to meat-lungs, ooph." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1040SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Solaris United? I don't know what that is. I'm gonna head back to work. Don't talk to me. Leave me alone." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1050SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Some legless repo mucker comes crawling up to me. 'Credits, Miss? Credits to me debt?'. Told him, 'Take a shift like the rest of us, I work for me self not the likes of you'." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker0960SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Want work? Gonna need a rebuild. Rebuild costs credits. Go into debt. Debt means they own ya. So you wanna be free? Gotta earn that now, means you gotta work more, more work needs more upgrades, more rebuilds. Means more debt, always more debt. Working me whole life to buy freedom from the debt I bought to buy the freedom to work, to buy me freedom! And we're so proud to be working for it. So stupid proud." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker0970SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "The only difference between a friend and an ambidexter is opportunity. There's not a person alive wouldn't sell you out for the right price." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker0980SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)

**B › Male Solaris**

- **Left of Rude Zuud**
- "Granddad's been dead for years. But we're still working to pay off his re-breathers, the kind with three-stage hexo-filtration. Pricey, but not much on resale. Some day we'll get clear, not in me lifetime, but maybe me kids. That's why I got me parts cheap, keep the overrates low, give 'em a shot at getting clear." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleDreams0540SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "For sure I upgraded me new toxin seals. Haven't yet paid off the old ones, mind. As me old man used to say, 'In a good cause, there are no failures, only delays'. Me lucky day's just around the corner." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleDreams0550SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Guts! Ain't ever gonna pay off me refit like this. The rates just keep going up!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleDreams0560SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Some of them Ostrons around? Why they leave a place like Cetus to come to the System's armpit, I got's no idea. Must like the smell of coolant. Nice enough folk though." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleGeneric0570SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Hah, no! She rejected him like a bad kidney! Ahh, there's nothing I wouldn't do for that woman." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleGeneric0580SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "I don't care about the badge. It's not right, and they're not getting away with it. Listen to your enemies, for the Void is beckonin'." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleGeneric0590SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Here's how you land where I'm at: Get to two bad days, drop off all your hope, and keep on driving." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRepo0450SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Tenno! Are they here because of that Oxium scam we pulled on Eris?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRepo0460SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "You don't mess with that group. It'd be like going up against your nightmares, and their lawyer. Can't wait to meet 'em? Check the guns." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRepo0470SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "That there rig jockey's abigail for Fastboy. Runs Hab 12. Fastboy gives a say-so, the abigail... he might not kill you, but you'll spend a cycle or two wishing he had. Know how to use those upgrades in ways the manufacturer never intended." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleScum0440SUMaleOne_en.ogg)

**C › Female Solaris**

- **Left of Rude Zuud**
- "Me brother got brain-shelved! Full repo (chuckling). Left me everything he had... which was debt. Fixed rate too, son of a bitch." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1210SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "That visitor? What talks in riddles? She stopped me the other day, thank me she did, for "that which I'm yet to do". I asked her what. She said: "Rewriting future history, by pausing a stranger." I suppose that's what I'm doing this moment, innit? (laughs) Oh.. oo I don't like this, I must get back to work." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1220SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I pay me seed every each and five cycles, straight outta me account. I make me good intentions. And know the Order of Profit safeguards me prosperity!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1230SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "What do you reckon it's like then... being brain-shelved? Just... dark, I reckon." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1240SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Got done bundled out a fat-boy bound for some pleasure dome. Mainly fabrics, lots of sealed containers. A few of them, well... a few of them were sobbing." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1250SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Word is we're shipping in some animals now. Some kinda safari park for Nef Anyo. I guess the resurrected native specimens weren't cutting it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1260SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "If you want me to work faster... It'd help me if I knew what I was looking for." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1270SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "The firestorms, the freezing temperatures, the toxic coolant, the wildlife... What do the Taxmen get outta this? Why are we doing this? Everything here wants us dead!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1280SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Seals failed around his chest-plate. I've guessed coolant got into his eyes. Down three days, he was. He's blind. Gonna be a long, hard road coming back from the interest on that one." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1290SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "(Humming)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleWorker1291SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)

**C › Male Solaris**

- **Directly across the coolant/sludge river from the Business**
- "Worked 70 years to get this far, and proud to say my body debt's half paid. Praise the Order! Life is profit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0650SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Me optics are gone, but I can't let on. I needs the work. Can't take the time off and sure as hell can't afford replacements. I'll just muddle through. Traffic control's easy enough work." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0660SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I have heard of places where offwordlers go to indulge their vices. You're a gambler? There are... tests of combat, held in... certain... outer dome worlds. They say the fighters bet all they have on themselves. If they win, they win great sums and... if they lose... well... it doesn't matter." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0670SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Warframes? We saw one once. Cost me a leg and me friends their lives. Fear... It is a sad life that is lived with its invisible curriculum." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0680SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I've got hazmat upgrades to take the risky work. Sleep upgrades so I can work more to pay off the upgrades. I make a payment, they say I ain't even cover the principle!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0690SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I could join the Order of Profit, sure! Donate half of what I earn and trust it'll come back to me tenfold, or... I could just grab a gun, right?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0700SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Yeah, rail agents. They're tractor jockeys. Solo contractors specializing in speed-runs, rapid deliveries, smuggling, stiletto jobs. They've cut more deals with more bureaucrats than you've had hot dinners. You'll know a rail agent tractor when you see it. Overclocked reactors, wide-bore engines, zero acceleration governors. Every design choice screams: death wish." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0710SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Oh, I know the rails, better than any of these muckers! Routes only I know, contacts only I have. I'm outta here. I've got the old man's tractor. I'll make me own way... as a rail agent!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0720SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Oh, we got plans, see? Our youngest, we're not getting him modded. No rig, see? Gonna use the money to get him educated, get him placed in the establishment. Twenty, maybe thirty years from now, we'll have family on the inside, doing us favors. That's how we're getting debt clear." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleWorker0730SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)

**D › Female Solaris**

- **South of Smokefinger**
- "You'll know him. He's an overly polite man with a gun tattooed on his neck. Someone that cluey might be able to integrate. We can't afford to pass on able hands. Not at the rate we breed." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRebel1120SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "I've got a bundled up fatboy and he deadheaded and empty by dinner time. They say you're an agent. You good for this?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleDispatcher1090SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Pumps on eastern pads low on go-go juice. Tractor's inbound, need a snappy refill. Come back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleDispatcher1080SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "'Holier than thou.' Had enough scratch to treat barons like concubines. That's who we're really working for, mark me words." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRailAgent1100SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Little Duck says she found something! A Void gate! That mean anything to you?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRailAgent1110SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Hah, keep telling yourself you're free, and you'll never escape. Working as intended am I right? (scoffs)" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleGeneric1150SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Me father went missing in the Grineer invasion on Mars! Perhaps you were there, Tenno? Perhaps you saw him?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleGeneric1160SUFemaleOne_en.ogg)

**D › Male Solaris**

- **Elevated room, entrance left of the Ventkids**
- "Affirmative. Triple digit rail agent needs action doc on the backstroke. Inbound with acceleration trauma. Come back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleDispatcher0390SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Till cart to Bay 12. Jockey blew out posterior hydraulics. Yeah... hero tried lifting above his grade." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleDispatcher0400SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Ah, guts! Tractor overhaul. Epsilon pad. Strip the battle rack, priority one. Rail agent needs to get it done yesterday!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleDispatcher0410SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Copy that, tractor, Sigma pad. Mercury outbound. Need a mounting shackle for version twelve on nubelist(?) to meet noon-time launch window." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleDispatcher0420SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Chek chek. I've got a long-haul tractor, bundled out. Needs a gimbal for a rotating gunnery hatch. Anyone got their ears on?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleDispatcher0430SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Made a delivery to a pleasure lake, once upon a time. Airships positioned to blow spore from fragrant forests, towards the Magnate's dining barge at suppertime. Me? I got a boot in the left quarter acutator for staring at his wife too long." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMalePilots0490SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "You live as a rail agent long enough, you see things that change you. I know a place. A hidden place. Home to creatures that never were. Species prevented from evolving by a rise of our own." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMalePilots0500SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "Ran a load of organics out to Cetus. They got a heavyweight Grineer problem. Copycats got the city pinned, and you don't even want to know what comes out of the lakes at night." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMalePilots0510SUMaleOne_en.ogg)
- "No, I'm not muckin' local. Tractor jockey, mate. Silver-plated Corpus contract. I'm up there. Hour this: I've got stars mate, I've got stars." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMalePilots0520SUMaleOne_en.ogg)

**E › Female Solaris**

- **Near the Orb Vallis elevator, at the side north of Eudico**
- "There's an agent down by the Spillway 19. Getcha' what ya want. Gib-faced mucker is a sidemouth ambidexter, though ever there was one. Don't tell her nothing you don't mind getting back to the taxman." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1300SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Welcome to the habs, kid. Nothing works, but anything can be arranged." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1310SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I know an action doc, gots a connection with a repo out for the Hab 12. Faulty parts, half-price! Bargain if you're handy with a solder and don't mind being a little twitchy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1320SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Eh, he's not too smart, but he can lift things." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1330SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "This cove's wooing me, he is. Unexpected visits, gifts of sealant, parts for that extra manipulator I wants installed. A romantic he is, a real fancy monger." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1340SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "What role this site played in Orokin history is uncertain, but the ties are clear." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1350SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Oh, my family don't worry 'bout me. I was never gonna make it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleScum1360SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)

**E › Male Solaris**

- **South of Legs**
- "Seen that character floating around, talking in riddles or not at all? I say she's one of them Quills. Where they go, things change, so they say. And when the things change, me mucker, it's folk like you and I get caught in the cogs." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleGeneric0860SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "You hail from Acheron? I have a brother there! Here, a gift for you! I'm not SELLING it, it is a GIFT! Thank you friend. You are most welcome. Say, might you loan me a couple of hundred credits? But, I have given you a gift! You would take that from me, and offer nothing in return? Criminal! Brigand! Thief!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleGeneric0870SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Me contact says they found a stash of Orokin era Nav charts. He tells me they mark sites that do not exist on current maps. Most intriguing. I'll pay you well, but you must submit to a memory wipe." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleGeneric0880SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Hahaha! Not to worry, plenty more more vertebra where that came from!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleGeneric0890SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I'm adrift, outta work, washed up. They can't take me coin, so they coming for me parts." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRepo0770SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Help a cove out, guv'ner. Buy bonds? I was a thrower, best in the biz. And I've been adrift for cycles. Buy bonds? 0.13% goes to paying off what little is left of me decrepit rig. Buy bonds? I don't clear the interest, I don't buy back me organs, It's brain-shelving for me for sure. Buy bonds, eh? Buy bonds?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRepo0840SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Repos. Work hard, or that's how you'll wind up. Arms repossessed, legs repossessed, eyes... repossessed. Work hard, earn your rig, and... you'll be fine." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRepo0850SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)

**F › Female Solaris**

- **Outside the backroom with Little Duck**
- "Hey! Friend! Spare a few credits?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRepo1410SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "There is another way! What they don't know is the only thing keeping me alive! Something's gonna fall like rain and it won't be flowers." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRebel1420SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Dictators, profiteers, sadists. These are souls that do not survive death." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRebel1430SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Hey! Smoke the brakes. They've avoided being picked up for repo for a fortnight now. They've learned the rules." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRepoAgent1460SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I've been a rig jockey, tractor jockey, rail agent, worked abigail for a slum executive more than once. Worked all kinds of jobs in all kinds of places. Never met a good person, or a bad one. It's just folks friend, everyone's just folks." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRailAgent1440SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Transported this Corpus once, got talkin'. Told me about the... this... what whatsit? 'Nominative determinism'. What you get named steers how you turn out. Maybe that's why the Corpus name some of us, not our kin. Is that loopy?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleRailAgent1450SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Piece of advice? Don't make no waves, don't bet no losers, and get off this backwater planet as soon as you can!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUFemaleGeneric1470SUFemaleTwo_en.ogg)

**F › Male Solaris**

- **Near the Orb Vallis elevator, south of Eudico, at the side with the Ostrons**
- "Merc-mercs! Mercantile Mercenaries! Corpus! Slick, and shiny, and a pain in me rear!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRailAgent0780SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Signed me tractor to a triple-digit convoy gone wide-ball for one of the Mercury asteroids. Emergency resupply. Got there way too late. Whole installation had gotten crater-faced, zits everywhere. Never seen so many heat-sinks. Flagged it as a no-go and left it to the merks. Infested. Damn, give me nightmares." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRailAgent0790SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I pilot a fatboy outta Mars, bundled off with some off-planet fauna. Something about a safari park." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRailAgent0800SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Rail agent? Ha! Jockeying meat wagons is where the money's at. Drop in, grab the breathers, and leg it. Easy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRailAgent0810SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "They'd headed me tractor two hours ago, empty. Sold the full manifest. Now it's just me and a bottle for the next cycle and a half, cheers!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRailAgent0820SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I can get ya half price on a full tank of go-go juice. Interested?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleRailAgent0830SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "I made quite a mistake with that captain. Not the kinda thing you can easily undo. When you've made peace with the world, then everything becomes a game, friend. Cetus thought me that." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleScum0740SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Lifting your eyes is bad business, friend. No percentage in shamming revolution, riling the establishment. Not if you want to stay on the right side of the grass. You'd best expect nothing from this life. Save the placing of one foot before the other." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleScum0750SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)
- "Get on the winning team! This place? It's isolated. The other Corpus? They want Nef Anyo to fail. That means ain't nobody gonna stop Solaris United takin' this whole place from him. Savvy?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUMaleScum0760SUMaleTwo_en.ogg)

**Female Ostron**

- "Offwordlers commonly think of themselves as one person, this is an error in thinking, surah. You are many people! This is why it is important to remain simple, so as to not confuse the many people you consist of." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUOstFemaleGeneric1660SUOstFemale_en.ogg)
- "Social pressure drives modification, modification drives profit, profit drives debt, debt drives control... These Solaris, they are all working to pay off their bodies, which they sold to the Corpus, to work for the Corpus, to pay off their bodies, which they have never owned! Ach, I weep for them." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUOstFemaleGeneric1670SUOstFemale_en.ogg)
- "I hear your words, but I see you in your silences." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUOstFemaleGeneric1680SUOstFemale_en.ogg)
- "The quieter I am, the more I hear. The I hear, the wiser I become. The wiser I become, the quieter I am." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUOstFemaleGeneric1690SUOstFemale_en.ogg)
- "If you want to change, accept who you are. Now will you reconsider what you are about to do?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUOstFemaleGeneric1700SUOstFemale_en.ogg)

**Ventkid**

- "Need a ride, glinty? K-Drives by the load! Just through that vent there! Hahaha!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:KubrodonVentkid1.ogg)
- "Check the vent, glinty! Just here! A paying customer's always welcome!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:KubrodonVentkid2.ogg)
- "Pobbers are smart, but for me, Kubrodons all the way! Flash, style, attitude. Ride godly, and be remembered!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:KubrodonVentkid3.ogg)
- "Vent Pobbers make it happen! Not Kubrodon flashy, but they can take anything stolen and turn it into a top` ride!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:KubrodonVentkid4.ogg)
- "Pobbers and Kubrodons, sure they go at each other, but it's all in fun. Only gotta hear us skegging it up together to know that for a fact!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:KubrodonVentkid5.ogg)

**Ventkid › Ambient**

- "Kubrodons! Kubrodons! Kubrodons!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:KubrodonVentkidAmbient.ogg)

**Broadcasts › Corpus PA**

- "Repo index: Dorm 4, eleven limbs (miscellaneous), three lungs, three sets optics, one loader." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0230SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Inbound tractors on reentry. Grower work available: squad rigs and reinforcement manipulators is in preference." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0240SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Rail agent, report to dispatch." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0250SUPA_en.ogg)
- "New arrivals. Due to high baseline atmospheric contaminants, all nourishment is killed by valve." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0270SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Clean up crews to spillways Zeta-4. Yellow alert." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0260SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Proprietary nutrient canisters compatible with all torso gastrostomy seals version 9 or later." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0280SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Ventilator installation above spillway Gamma-9. Foot traffic reroute via habway 7-7-Beta." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0290SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Canals 1 through 4 at 72%. Canal locks operational. Coolant throughput nominal. Carry on." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0300SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Shift change. Time to change shifts. Shift change." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0310SUPA_en.ogg)

**Broadcasts › Propaganda Female Broadcaster**

- "Need quick credits to cover that sudden emergency? The Order of Profit provides fast loans to the faithful! Minimum net pay requirements supply may vary per applicant. Other conditions may also apply. The Order of Profit may alter agreement at any time to include, but is not restricted to: servitude, manual labor, generational indenture, organ donation, mind-wipe, brain-shelving, genetic experimentation, and death. Ask an Order representative for more information." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0080SUPA_en.ogg)
- "It's cold outside. Warm yourself with the teaching of the Order of Profit. Rates apply." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0090SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Had some bad luck? Need a financial boost to cover the interest? With over 300 repayment models, we're sure to assign one that's right for you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0100SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Life is profit, profit is life." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0110SUPA_en.ogg)
- "You work hard, and deserve better. Our installment plan options provide peace of mind while keeping you happy and housed in the rig of your dreams." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0120SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Success: it's not for losers." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0130SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Increase your usual donation to Corpus Solaris Order of Profit. A larger seed makes for a larger harvest. It just makes sense!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0140SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Corpus: looking out for YOU, as we have always done." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0160SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Be on the lookout for non-conformists. Accurate reports earn substantial rewards!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0170SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Use downtime effectively. Rest, and put in a solid shift's work tomorrow." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0180SUPA_en.ogg)
- "A few days off? Interest accrues. Now you're in a fight you cannot win. Who can you turn to? The Corpus Solaris Order of Profit. Plant a seed today! Life is profit, profit is life." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0190SUPA_en.ogg)
- "The street corner wretch, lacking sensors, limbs, dignity. Weeks away from brain-shelving. The wages of indolence. Fight proudly for your body Solaris, and work." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0200SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Charity encourages laziness. Do not donate to charity. Direct your financial compassion to the Order of Profit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0210SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Each cycle, ask yourself: 'Can I work just one percent harder?' The answer is always: yes!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1860SUPA_en.ogg)

**Broadcasts › Propaganda Male Broadcaster**

- "You may qualify for pay-cycle advances at newly reduced rates. Inquire today!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0070SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Heads up workers! You're one payday closer to financial freedom." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0150SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Limb and organ repossession happens. Safe guard your most valuable asset: YOU. Purchase repo insurance today." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem0220SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Long day in Fortuna, huh? Need to downcycle and feel good? Reward yourself with a donation to the Temple of Profit. Feel the Prosperity!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1800SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Good news! You still have jobs. Carry on." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1810SUPA_en.ogg)
- "What is interest, if not a measure of Nef's trust in you. What is the bodily repossession, if not the mark of one who cannot be trusted? What is it to betray the trust of he who has made a place for you by his side? It is to be unworthy of the name: 'Solaris'. Head high, workers of Fortuna, and be worthy." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1820SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Tired hands are honest hands." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1830SUPA_en.ogg)
- "A friendly reminder that consorting with anti-Temple dissidents carries a non-negotiable penalty of full-body repossession and mandatory brain-shelving. Report any suspicious personnel for a 0.63% remuneration bonus! Keep Fortuna clean." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1840SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Nef Anyo would like to congratulate those who accepted mandatory cleanup duty in the toxin sumps. Enjoy your 2 percent rebate on lung replacement." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1850SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Nef's table is set, and your chair awaits high above. How long will you keep him waiting?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1870SUPA_en.ogg)
- "There is no failure, only indolence. There is no futility, only parasitism. There is no defeat, only sloth." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1880SUPA_en.ogg)
- "Fortuna, a message from the teachings of the Temple of Profit. With an open heart: give. With a tranquil mind: give. With conscience clear and unburdened: give. Give, that Nef Anyo might smile upon you, and all the work you put your manipulators to. Give. Give. Give." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DSUPASystem1890SUPA_en.ogg)

**Broadcasts › Nef Anyo**

- "Nef Anyo here. Like you, I toiled, friends, I toiled, but I pulled myself up, dusted myself off, and purchased my future. Join me here, in orbit, out of the dirt, above everyone else. It's just... better." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral1.ogg)
- "There is a place for you within the Order of Profit, visitor. Speak to one of our evangelists today! The Void be the word, and the word be profit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral2.ogg)
- "You desire riches, friend? Well, let me tell you: so does your neighbor. What to do? Desire. RICHES. More than your neighbor, MORE than your brother, MORE THAN YOUR OVERSEER! Desire, friend, will raise you from the muck! Will raise you to the stars! Will raise you to your rightful place with the faithful, here, in orbit, by my side. Tell me, will I see you soon?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral3.ogg)
- "The Void be the word, and the word be profit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral4.ogg)
- "Solaris, are you listening? Know that I have plans for you. Know that the Order of Profit safeguards your hope, your well-being, and our shared future. Donate today, with the credits in your account, and the sweat of your brow. Give." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral5.ogg)
- "Clasp hands with us. The Order of Profit is home to all. Clasp hands, and know the ecstasy of a strong return on your faith." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral6.ogg)
- "Life is profit. Profit is life." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral7.ogg)
- "Donate your seed, that the Order of Profit may nurture that seed to a bountiful crop! A crop that you may harvest your faith, returned to you tenfold! The larger your seed, the greater your reward, the stronger we all become for your wisdom. Donate. Harvest. Grow. Now." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral8.ogg)
- "Give unto The Void, and be rewarded." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral9.ogg)
- "Give unto The Void, and be rewarded a hundredfold! Clasp hands with the Order of Profit. Plant that seed, and watch together as it grows into a mighty tree! Such a broad trunk! Such deep, strong roots! And behold's broad and mighty branches, with which it collects the bountiful rains of fortune. Life is profit! Profit is life! The Void be the word, and the word be profit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral10.ogg)
- "Venus restoration is not progressing fast enough! To lighten your workload, I have instituted a small interest rate hike, which compounds at each cycle. As ever, good and faithful Solaris, the Order's flock receive 15% toward this debt amalgamation! Profit is life. Plan today." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral11.ogg)
- "I was once like you. Poor. Indebted. Lazy. Then, the Void spoke to me. It said: 'Work harder'. And so, I did. I earned what I have. And now, Fortuna, it's your turn. Listen to the Void. Work. Harder." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral12.ogg)
- "Are you in debt, Solaris? Then you need to push yourself. Work harder, longer. The Void does not give unto the freeloader." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral13.ogg)
- "Nef Anyo, speaking directly to you. Well done, members of the Corpus Solaris Order of Profit. I have this cycle's figures, and they are very pleasing! I embrace you all." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral14.ogg)
- "Nef Anyo's Order of Profit welcomes you, offworld philantropists and entrepreneurs. The Void be the word, and the word be profit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NefAnyoFortunaGeneral15.ogg)

**Broadcasts › Vox Solaris**

- "No other species works for autonomy. No other species works to own their own body. No other species takes such pride in. their. enslavement. Solaris divided, we are slaves. Solaris UNITED, we are a force! The future can be different. Believe. Resist." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:VoxEntityFortuna1.ogg)
- "Solaris. United. You are not alone." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:VoxEntityFortuna2.ogg)
- "The Temple profits from your labor. Without your labor, then, there is no profit. What is the Temple of Profit without 'profit'? What then are the Corpus. without. 'us'?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:VoxEntityFortuna3.ogg)
- "HOW does the Taxman keep YOU under his boot? Deny you that which you need? Blame the poor for it, and then tell you: 'Working even harder will fix it'?!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:VoxEntityFortuna4.ogg)
- "The Taxman teaches that truth plus profit equals life. Solaris United reminds you that, if this is true, then truth is life without profit, and profit is life without truth." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:VoxEntityFortuna5.ogg)
- "How often have you sat in The Temple of Profit as your parts fail and your families starve, looked up to all that gold and grandeur and wonder if you've been had?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:VoxEntityFortuna6.ogg)

### Nightwave/Amir's Shockwave/Quotes

[全セリフ](nightwave-amir-s-shockwave.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Nightwave/Amir's_Shockwave/Quotes)

**Amir's Shockwave › Radio Chatter**

- Amir: [sound of things being knocked over] "Sorry! Sorry. I... I saw a spider." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0430Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Höllvania Bowl. Where the ball never stops rolling... Until we get hit by a technozombie apocalypse. Ball stopped rolling pretty quick after that. Yeeeeah." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0440Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Come on down to Twisty Top, where we have 8,762 flavors of soft serve, like "Techrot", "Grass", and "Oh Sol, Why Is It Savory?"" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0450Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You're now listening to... 102.3 Real Rock FM Where we play nothin' but rock... ...rock... ....and more ROCK!!! This ain't your granny's station!" (subtitle appears: Now Playing - The Great Despair by On-lyne) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0570Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Sometimes I think about how many of you are listening to me. And then I panic and wee myself a little." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0580Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You ever just sit in your Orbiter and think about stuff? No? Cool cool cool cool cool cool cool cool." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0640Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "If there's a long pause, it's not for dramatic effect. I just tripped over the cord again." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0690Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Coming to you from a Capitalist Hellscape in a room with no windows! By choice! Mostly!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0700Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "If you're sitting in the dark alone at 3 A.M. — first of all, SAMESIES. Second of all, drink some water. Seriously. Hydrate." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0710Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "If you're looking for the good shrooms, you gotta go underground. Trust me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0720Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Word on the street - well under the street - is that there's a futuristic city sitting on top of something REAL interesting. Caps off to whoever finds it first. Get it? Caps? Like mushroom - okay moving on." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0730Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Say "Roots & Shoots" one more time, I dare you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0740Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You know what they say: When one door closes, a weiiiiird tunnel to a mushroom trip opens up underneath a futuristic city. Nobody says that? Huh." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0750Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "My mom used to say my laundry would grow mushrooms if I didn't air it out. I wonder if that's what happened in the Deepmines..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0760Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "... What the Hell is a Sprodling?! WAIT... WAIT I WANT ONE. DRIFTER - BRING ME BACK A SPRODLING RIGHT NOW. RIGHT. NOW." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0770Amir_en.ogg)
- Arthur: "Amir! For the last time, get off the comms!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0410Arthur_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You're not my real dad!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0420Amir_en.ogg)
- Aoi: "Please?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0460Aoi_en.ogg)
- Amir: "No." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0470Amir_en.ogg)
- Aoi: "Pleeeeeease?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0480Aoi_en.ogg)
- Amir: "NO. Fiiiiine. Listeners, please enjoy this 48-hour uninterrupted block of On-lyne. Brought to you by Aoi." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0490Amir_en.ogg)
- Aoi: "Thank you, Amir! MUAH." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0500Aoi_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Yeah, yeah... you're welcome." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0510Amir_en.ogg)
- Vay Hek: "Report! Tell me it's done. Tell me you killed the Tenno scoom." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0520VayHek_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Nah, I got a double meatball sub 'n soda down here for a Haywood, last name... Jablomi?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0530Amir_en.ogg)
- Vay Hek: "Huh. Oh. Hey. Which one of you festering tube rats is Haywood Jablomi? GRINEER! HAY-WOOD-JA-BLOW-ME? Nobody?! Wait..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0540VayHek_en.ogg)
- Amir: [delighted cackling] [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0550Amir_en.ogg)
- Vay Hek: "Tenno. When I find you I'm going to-" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0560VayHek_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Hey Quincy! C'mere!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0590Amir_en.ogg)
- Quincy: "Nah bruv, listen, keep me outta whatever mental-" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0600Quincy_en.ogg)
- Amir: "I have the whole of time and space listening to us. Right here. C'mooooon. You know you wanna say something." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0610Amir_en.ogg)
- Quincy: "We may never meet. You may be bleedin' out there, on some rock in space, somewhere we ain't ever gonna see. But know this: Your back ain't against the wall, that wall's holdin' you up, fam. And it's got four numbers painted across it. Nineteen-ninety-nine. We got you. Hold strong." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0620Quincy_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Well... shit." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0630Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Yeah, Eleanor? Oh. No, they can't. No I have no idea what'll happen if you-HNNHGHKKK—" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0650Amir_en.ogg)
- Eleanor: "I'm coming to you live across the unfathomable aeons. Among all the chaos and the bloodshed, it's easy to forget the tender touches that make it all worthwhile. Love yourself, embrace yourself, and above all, accept yourself, or so help me I'll give you a tongue-lashing you'll never forget. Stay shiny. All my love to the lady with the tentacle hat." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0660Eleanor_en.ogg)
- Lettie: "AMIR! Stop playing with yourself, dinner's ready." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0670Lettie_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Eee!! You're on your own, listeners!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0680Amir_en.ogg)

**Unused/Removed › Höllvania Central Mall 2024 Demo Idle Quotes**

- "This place used to smell so good. Coffee. Cookies. Fresh clean socks. Now it's just rust, pain and old socks." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DBarks0110Jabir_en.ogg)
