- Dominus Thrax: "This hovering fortress should be mine. Are the skies over Duviri not part of Duviri?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0010DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "Such a wretched place to be raised in." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0020DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "Automated servants! How cheap. Though... loyal." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0030DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "This is far enough. I shall manifest no further in. Not without my Centurions." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0040DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "Think not that I stand alone! My legions would come if I summoned them!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0050DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "For years this place has mocked me from above, and now through sheer force of will, I have reached it!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0060DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "I claim this proud steel bastion in the name of Thrax. Perhaps next I'll venture beyond the wall." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0070DomThrax_en.ogg)
- Dominus Thrax: "How Bombastine will envy me for this bold expedition." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DProxBarks0080DomThrax_en.ogg)

### Operation: Gargoyle's Cry/Quotes

[全セリフ](operation-gargoyle-s-cry.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Operation:_Gargoyle's_Cry/Quotes)

**Vigile Jahu Gargoyle › Idle**

- Fibonacci: "One hears stories of demented architects. One does not expect to admire their work first-hand." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0400Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Do please remember that I can see everything that goes on in this Dojo. EVERYTHING." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0410Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "You should invite some friends to explore this jolly maze you've built. Based on Escher was it?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0420Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Why not ask some guests from another Clan over to admire your handiwork? Think of how jealous they would be of your Gargoyle." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0430Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Decluttering. Ever heard of it?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0440Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "My humble tank is a palace compared to some Dojos I've seen. Real shit holes." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0450Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Who picked out the color scheme, and are they wanted for any additional offenses?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0460Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Thank you so much for bringing me into your home. You know, it's not quite as squalid as I had feared." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0470Fib_en.ogg)
- Fibonacci: "Were you aiming for a sort of... rococo... effect?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DGargEventShop0480Fib_en.ogg)

### Operation: Orphix Venom/Quotes

[全セリフ](operation-orphix-venom.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Operation:_Orphix_Venom/Quotes)

**In the Necralisk › Event Offerings › Idle**

- Father: "There were four of us. Barroc, Kailen, Mutaru, and me, patching ruptures on the a rust bucket of a Railjack off the Delta Proxima. I remember our first sight of a Sentient Murex... Eh, maybe some other time." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0050Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "One day you're a Railjack gunner, riding high and proud. The next you're a broken man on a Xenorhast bier, telling war stories to Tenno who politely pretend to listen." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0060Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "I'm not sure how long I can keep a lid on the mech project. She hasn't said anything, but I'm sure she suspects." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0070Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "You tried that Bonewidow yet? One of the most fearsome tools I've ever constructed. Cold, cutting, ruthlessly efficient. Based her on my wife." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0080Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "Ballas, huh? Who would have known. Who... would... have... known." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0090Father_en.ogg)
- Father: "I think my wife's clinging on to the idea we'll all just jump into new bodies one day. Would be nice. She sure made a mess of mine." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventBrowseStore0100Father_en.ogg)

**During Orphix Venom Missions › Random Transmissions**

- Natah: "Deploying squadron I-0-5 to attack vectors. Do not let their Old War relics interfere." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0170Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Orphix launch reinforcement across all lanes. Delta sequence A-1." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0160Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "M-2 protocol in effect. We require sector control. Do not let them advance." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0180Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "All systems showing D-3 degregation. Did not anticipate Necramechs, but we will adapt." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0190Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Adapt next series. Deploying from Y-4 quadrant. We can not be stopped." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0200Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Orphix N-6 series lost. Adjusting repeater deployment algorithm." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0210Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Entrati interference must be cross-controlled. Segment G-7 restarting." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0220Natah_en.ogg)
- The arranged letters spell out "I AM DYING" in the English localization of the game. Different localizations of the game will have their own version of the phrase spelled out (e.g in French the letters spell out "JE MEURS").

**During Orphix Venom Missions › Random Transmissions › Direct**

- Natah: "I speak for myself. No. I am the hollow vessel. The echo." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0100Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Sentient as we, as they are not. Simple. It is not simple." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0110Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "The steel vipers sleep now, I showed them how. I.... had my reasons." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0120Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "I sing. No. The wind sings my emptiness. I am the queen of nothing." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0130Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Unraveled, the serpent coiling of their genes. The secrete phrases of cell and cipher. I too am encoiled." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0140Natah_en.ogg)
- Natah: "Who woke the Orphix? Who sang the steel lullaby to the viperous children? Who?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMechEventInbox0150Natah_en.ogg)

### Operation: Scarlet Spear/Quotes

[全セリフ](operation-scarlet-spear.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Operation:_Scarlet_Spear/Quotes)

**In Scarlet Spear Flotilla Relay › Idle**

- Little Duck: "Grineer. I'd trust a tubers' response to this fiasco about as far as a could spit one." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0050LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Look, the Taxmen ain't coming. Last I've check, the Board was still debating whether or not this was an actual threat." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0060LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Each one of their Murex type in info back to deep space and that's our in." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0070LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "The Quills must of seen this coming but said nothing. Why? We are gonna have words." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0080LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Konzu? Konzu? Mate, if you don't redirect some of the talent this way, lunch will be the least of your problems. Little Duck out." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0090LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Ground teams this way. Railjack crews that way. Move!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0100LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Yeah Eudi, anyone you can spare. Yeah. Yeah it is. Gotta go, someone's here." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0110LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Step right up my vengeant angels. We got a system to save! Sign here love." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0120LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "They've concentrated in the Veil for now. But not for long. Mark my words." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0130LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "They're firing off Condrix on rays of recon missions, building up situation data for a big push. We gotta hit this in the neck before they fire off that info back home." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0140LittleDuck_en.ogg)
- Little Duck: "Hey, your crew did good. Get some rest. You're back on deck in five." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DRelay0150LittleDuck_en.ogg)

### Orbiter

[全セリフ](orbiter.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Orbiter)

**Upper Section (Bridge) › General Radio Chatter**

- View Radio Transmission List▾▾
- "Incoming punch. All ships maintain holding pattern." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio1.ogg)
- "Galleon C-tok-tok (C22), prepare for boarding inspection." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio2.ogg)
- "Tok-chee-ke-chee-ke (233), hold postion; you do not have authorization." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio3.ogg)
- "Attention all ships, uhh, detecting echoes. Stay alert, could be caused by Tenno void masks." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio4.ogg)
- "Tenno ship detected. Ready flak cannons! Shuk! RAMN! Lost it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio5.ogg)
- "Uhh... Control, uk-shux-chee uk-chee (163-13) checks out. Yeah, clear for punch." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio6.ogg)
- "Ke-tok-ke, enter the queue, stand by for punch. Ke-tok-ke, Mercury rail is ready to receive. Proceed. Glory to our Queens." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio7.ogg)
- "The Tenno capture warrant has been upgraded. All Tenno are to be exterminated." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio8.ogg)
- "All ships hold, the rail is punching through. Rail charging, continue to hold. Rail over capacity, please divert into holding pattern." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio9.ogg)
- "Bre-nek-tok-transport, broadcast your passage visa now. Passage visa accepted. Bre-nek-tok, you're free to go. Hail to the Queens." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio10.ogg)
- "The Tenno awakening must not interfere with Grineer liberation of the system." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio11.ogg)
- "Is this your entire cargo? Wai- what's that? Do you have a passage visa for this, uhh, Forma? ...Uh-huh, very well. For the Queens." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio12.ogg)
- "Tenno sightings near Mars have been confirmed. Lancer battalions are now being mobilized to the area." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio13.ogg)
- "The Tenno threat is always present. Eternal alertness is the duty of every Grineer." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio14.ogg)
- "Transport shux-chee-uk (631), dock for inspection. Accepting rail arrivals now. Welcome back, captain. Proceed to dock for customs and inspection. Next in queue. Power. Punch." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio15.ogg)
- "Fin-kan-ehught-tok-corvette, transmit your travel pass immediately. My apologies Councilor, uhh, Fin-kan-ehught-tok-corvette, you may proceed freely. Glory to the Queens." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio16.ogg)
- "Uhh, attention enforcement squad six leader, board and search the incoming transport. Illegal foodstuffs suspected. If found, execute the standard punishments, copy?" "Aye-aye, for the Queens." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio17.ogg)
- "Our agreements with the Corpus only go so far. The aggressive actions by Alad V will not be tolerated." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio18.ogg)
- "Corpus transport ehught-chee-ehught-chee (8383), stand by for Infestation scan. Corpus transport ehught-chee-ehught-chee, scans indicate Infestation, Code Yellow. Prepare your organic crewmen for decontamination." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio19.ogg)
- "Councilor Vay Hek has passed his Fomorian initiative. These ships will help ensure Grineer power remains unchallenged." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio20.ogg)
- "All units alert, Status Yellow. All scanners full power, now. Tenno unmasking, ready flak. Wait, cease fire, CEASE FIRE! Fe-be-tok is already close to the rail. Hold fire! RAMN! Gone." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio21.ogg)
- "Hmm... manifest, check. Passage visa, check. Hull integrity, heh! Check. Tenno energy residue, hmm... negligible. Yeah, you're authorized for punch captain." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio22.ogg)
- "A warrant has been issued for the rogue Corpus smuggler known only as Darvo. This individual is thought to be behind a rash of thefts and hijackings." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio23.ogg)
- "Uk-forr-uk (141), please advise, detecting void mask echoes. Tenno ships converging on your signal. Uk-forr-uk, we are still seeing void echoes, announce Tenno contact immediately. Uk-forr-uk, come in. ...Uk-forr-uk, respond." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio24.ogg)
- "By order of the Queens, Admiral Vor will lead the Tenno execution program. Vor has vowed to destroy the Tenno before they awake." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio25.ogg)
- "The Corpus have finally agreed to our demands. The Grineer will now have access to technologies that further our fight in the Outer Sectors." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio26.ogg)
- "Ehught-nhune-ehught (898), drop bearing seven-point-three, reduce speed. Ehught-nhune-ehught, you have taken severe hull damage to your ship. Do you have casualties to declare? Ehught-nhune-ehught, understood. Stand-by for troop replenishment. Sending repair drones now. For the glory of the Queens." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio27.ogg)
- "Due to his formidable performance crushing the insubordination of Saturn, Sargas Ruk has been promoted to the rank of General." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio28.ogg)
- "Galleon-mark-fhuve, prepare for jump. Galleon-mark-fhuve, hold position. Detecting anomalies. N-Fhuve-Fhuve (N55), do you detect similar? Galleon-mark-fhuve... Ramn. Tenno ship detected, attack. They're gone. Argh, Bek." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio29.ogg)
- "Today the mining colonies of Saturn welcomed Grineer troops as liberators. Rebel leaders were executed to launch public celebrations of this momentous occasion." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio30.ogg)
- "Grineer forces in the Outer Terminus have become increasingly distracted by encounters with the Tenno (???). Councilor Vay Hek's Fomorian ship initiative is soon to restore order to the area." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio31.ogg)
- "You, show me your passage visa. Next! You. Visa. Now. ...Huh, what's this? Visa expired? Take him! Control, prepare another confinement transport. Listen! Ignorance is no excuse for an expired document! Next!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio32.ogg)
- "Huh, next... next! Yeah, you! Mm-hmm, c'mere. Passage visa? Uhh, yeah. Next... next! Passage visa? Ffuuh... how many more...? Really? Ramn. Next!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio33.ogg)
- "Uhh... we're entering the unresponsive vessel. Huhh, no signs of Infestation so far. Ramn, there are casualties. Mm-hmm, everywhere. It looks like, uhh, blade weapons. Yeah. Ra-pat-tok! Uh-huh, Tenno skoom! Yeah, they're all dead. Mark this ship for repopulation." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio34.ogg)
- "Uhh, this is, uhh-uhh, this is irregular. Orders!? From who!? Let me see your authorization! Uhhh... alright... Yeah, I need to call this one in. Control, confirm order designation, uhh, shux-ehught-ehught (688)." "Authorization confirmed, stand down enforcement leader." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio35.ogg)
- "Show me your ID cards now! You! Where's your ramn!? Down on the ground! You! C'mere! That better not be a Red Veil badge. Grab him. Search those crates! Duhh... rail leader, requesting ship lockdown. Perform deep scan for Tenno and Tenno sympathizers. You! I'm placing you in custody!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio36.ogg)
- "Uhh, captain, do you know uh any Tenno been on this ship? There's, uhh, energy residue everywhere. Yeah, yeah, scan your system; look for uh signs of intrusion." "Yeah, you understand, uhh, this is gonna need to be reported to, uhh, High Command. Uh-huh. Yeah, well, this is, uhh, very serious. Vor will want to know." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio37.ogg)
- "Attention Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee-Shux (53436), your transport manifest does not indicate additional lifeforms, do you have anything else to declare? Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee-Shux, are you aware of the punishment for false declarations? Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee-Shux, do you copy? Fhuve-Chee! Gretesk! We've got a runner! Enforcement drones, deploy. Target vessel Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee. Suspected smuggler Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee eliminated. Glory to the Queens." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio38.ogg)
- "Rail Control, this is merchant transport 3398 reporting for passage. [pause] Rail Control, I don't see why boarding would be necessary. Perhaps if I sent over a sample container of my wares, you could inspect that? I mean, you wouldn't even need to send it back. [pause] Okay, okay. How about I send over... five containers for... uh, inspection? [pause] Ah, a pleasure doing business with you, Rail Control." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:Container_inspection.ogg)
- "Command, we've breached the ship. What is growing all over the walls and floors? Disgusting! It looks like it's... alive? [pause] No command, no sign of any personnel. Requesting orders. How do we proceed? Hold on a second... I think I heard something." [infested scream and several gunshots] [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:InfestedShipInvestigation.ogg)
- "Command, this is Grustrag transport uk-tok(12), standby for delivery of cargo. Docking now. Oh, and if you know what's good for you, you'll prepare a full security detail in receiving. (sigh) Can't wait for these monsters to be your problem, and not mine." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:Delivering.ogg)

- "The Corpus have finally agreed to our demands. The Grineer will now have access to technologies that further our fight in the Outer Sectors." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio26.ogg)
- "Ehught-nhune-ehught (898), drop bearing seven-point-three, reduce speed. Ehught-nhune-ehught, you have taken severe hull damage to your ship. Do you have casualties to declare? Ehught-nhune-ehught, understood. Stand-by for troop replenishment. Sending repair drones now. For the glory of the Queens." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio27.ogg)
- "Due to his formidable performance crushing the insubordination of Saturn, Sargas Ruk has been promoted to the rank of General." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio28.ogg)
- "Galleon-mark-fhuve, prepare for jump. Galleon-mark-fhuve, hold position. Detecting anomalies. N-Fhuve-Fhuve (N55), do you detect similar? Galleon-mark-fhuve... Ramn. Tenno ship detected, attack. They're gone. Argh, Bek." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio29.ogg)
- "Today the mining colonies of Saturn welcomed Grineer troops as liberators. Rebel leaders were executed to launch public celebrations of this momentous occasion." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio30.ogg)
- "Grineer forces in the Outer Terminus have become increasingly distracted by encounters with the Tenno (???). Councilor Vay Hek's Fomorian ship initiative is soon to restore order to the area." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio31.ogg)
- "You, show me your passage visa. Next! You. Visa. Now. ...Huh, what's this? Visa expired? Take him! Control, prepare another confinement transport. Listen! Ignorance is no excuse for an expired document! Next!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio32.ogg)
- "Huh, next... next! Yeah, you! Mm-hmm, c'mere. Passage visa? Uhh, yeah. Next... next! Passage visa? Ffuuh... how many more...? Really? Ramn. Next!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio33.ogg)
- "Uhh... we're entering the unresponsive vessel. Huhh, no signs of Infestation so far. Ramn, there are casualties. Mm-hmm, everywhere. It looks like, uhh, blade weapons. Yeah. Ra-pat-tok! Uh-huh, Tenno skoom! Yeah, they're all dead. Mark this ship for repopulation." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio34.ogg)
- "Uhh, this is, uhh-uhh, this is irregular. Orders!? From who!? Let me see your authorization! Uhhh... alright... Yeah, I need to call this one in. Control, confirm order designation, uhh, shux-ehught-ehught (688)." "Authorization confirmed, stand down enforcement leader." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio35.ogg)
- "Show me your ID cards now! You! Where's your ramn!? Down on the ground! You! C'mere! That better not be a Red Veil badge. Grab him. Search those crates! Duhh... rail leader, requesting ship lockdown. Perform deep scan for Tenno and Tenno sympathizers. You! I'm placing you in custody!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio36.ogg)
- "Uhh, captain, do you know uh any Tenno been on this ship? There's, uhh, energy residue everywhere. Yeah, yeah, scan your system; look for uh signs of intrusion." "Yeah, you understand, uhh, this is gonna need to be reported to, uhh, High Command. Uh-huh. Yeah, well, this is, uhh, very serious. Vor will want to know." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio37.ogg)
- "Attention Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee-Shux (53436), your transport manifest does not indicate additional lifeforms, do you have anything else to declare? Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee-Shux, are you aware of the punishment for false declarations? Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee-Shux, do you copy? Fhuve-Chee! Gretesk! We've got a runner! Enforcement drones, deploy. Target vessel Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee. Suspected smuggler Fhuve-Chee-Forr-Chee eliminated. Glory to the Queens." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:LisetRadio38.ogg)
- "Rail Control, this is merchant transport 3398 reporting for passage. [pause] Rail Control, I don't see why boarding would be necessary. Perhaps if I sent over a sample container of my wares, you could inspect that? I mean, you wouldn't even need to send it back. [pause] Okay, okay. How about I send over... five containers for... uh, inspection? [pause] Ah, a pleasure doing business with you, Rail Control." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:Container_inspection.ogg)
- "Command, we've breached the ship. What is growing all over the walls and floors? Disgusting! It looks like it's... alive? [pause] No command, no sign of any personnel. Requesting orders. How do we proceed? Hold on a second... I think I heard something." [infested scream and several gunshots] [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:InfestedShipInvestigation.ogg)
- "Command, this is Grustrag transport uk-tok(12), standby for delivery of cargo. Docking now. Oh, and if you know what's good for you, you'll prepare a full security detail in receiving. (sigh) Can't wait for these monsters to be your problem, and not mine." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:Delivering.ogg)

**Nora Night**

- View Nora Night List▾▾
- "A voice in the night, a half-remembered dream, rising to the surface of your consciousness, from backbrain to forebrain, a sound to a vision, pullin' up and... knockity-knock. Hello, Dreamers. Let's get to know one another." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight1.ogg)
- "To all of you driftin' out there in the black, mark the frequency. The time has come to act together. To open our eyes and gaze with utmost clarity past the present and into a future we create. Shoulder-to-shoulder. Mark the frequency." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight2.ogg)
- "An echo. From long ago: 'For ten years I have been polishing this sword. Its frosty edge has never been put to the test. Now, I am holding it and showing it to you, sir: Is there anyone suffering from injustice?' Keep livin', Dreamers." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight3.ogg)
- "Hey there, Dreamers. You hearin' me? I know you are. No need to say anythin'. A hand in yours, a voice for the speechless, a bedtime story for the sleepless. The name's Nora Night, and I got somethin' to say, sweet things. Nightwave is coming." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight4.ogg)
- "It can be tough as a kid. All sorts of people have it over you. People will kill you inside, kill you and forget your name. You grow up. They grow old. You remember. They don't. They just keep doing what they've always done. Only to someone else. Then, one day, they call you friend. And you wait. And you wait. You wait 'til they can't trust you any more than they do. And then you ask them if they enjoyed their dinner. And, looking into their panicked eyes as they gasp their last, you tell them your name. And you nod. 'Yeah', your eyes say to theirs. 'That was me'. And then you leave. And the System is a better place. Be smart, Dreamers." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight5.ogg)
- "In a world like this, it can be hard to have hope, when the man owns the system and the system serves to save the man, from us. But I'm here, Dreamers. To help you pierce that false fog. We'll chase it away with acts of beauty and - succeed or fail - face the foe, eyes open. As someone once said: If nothing saves us from death, may love at least save us from life." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight6.ogg)
- "Hey there, Dreamers. The Devil is home and the shades are up. That's right... it's just you and me, passin' each other slow and with a nod sayin' all that needs sayin'. I'm Nora Night, sayin'... you live with wolves you better act like one." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight7.ogg)
- "Acts of defiance, acts of generosity, acts of sacrifice... this is how we turn this system 'round. I'll take you there, Dreamers. I'll bring you back." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight8.ogg)
- "Nora here. The Red King is at the dance. But listen... as you drift between the stars Nora wants you to know you ain't alone. Mm-mm. We're all of us a kinda family out here, ain't we? All of us. The multitudes. Driftin' and listenin'. But you need to remember: It never troubles the wolf how many the sheep may be. It never. Troubles. The Wolf." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:NoraNight9.ogg)

- Amir: [sound of things being knocked over] "Sorry! Sorry. I... I saw a spider." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0430Amir_en.ogg)

- Amir: "You're now listening to... 102.3 Real Rock FM Where we play nothin' but rock... ...rock... ....and more ROCK!!! This ain't your granny's station!" (subtitle appears: Now Playing - The Great Despair by On-lyne) [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0570Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Sometimes I think about how many of you are listening to me. And then I panic and wee myself a little." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0580Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You ever just sit in your Orbiter and think about stuff? No? Cool cool cool cool cool cool cool cool." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0640Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "If there's a long pause, it's not for dramatic effect. I just tripped over the cord again." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0690Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Coming to you from a Capitalist Hellscape in a room with no windows! By choice! Mostly!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0700Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "If you're sitting in the dark alone at 3 A.M. — first of all, SAMESIES. Second of all, drink some water. Seriously. Hydrate." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0710Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "If you're looking for the good shrooms, you gotta go underground. Trust me." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0720Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Word on the street - well under the street - is that there's a futuristic city sitting on top of something REAL interesting. Caps off to whoever finds it first. Get it? Caps? Like mushroom - okay moving on." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0730Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "Say "Roots & Shoots" one more time, I dare you." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0740Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You know what they say: When one door closes, a weiiiiird tunnel to a mushroom trip opens up underneath a futuristic city. Nobody says that? Huh." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0750Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "My mom used to say my laundry would grow mushrooms if I didn't air it out. I wonder if that's what happened in the Deepmines..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0760Amir_en.ogg)
- Amir: "... What the Hell is a Sprodling?! WAIT... WAIT I WANT ONE. DRIFTER - BRING ME BACK A SPRODLING RIGHT NOW. RIGHT. NOW." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DNoraNokkoHint0770Amir_en.ogg)
- Arthur: "Amir! For the last time, get off the comms!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0410Arthur_en.ogg)
- Amir: "You're not my real dad!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0420Amir_en.ogg)
- Aoi: "Please?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0460Aoi_en.ogg)

- Amir: "NO. Fiiiiine. Listeners, please enjoy this 48-hour uninterrupted block of On-lyne. Brought to you by Aoi." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0490Amir_en.ogg)
- Aoi: "Thank you, Amir! MUAH." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0500Aoi_en.ogg)

- Amir: "Nah, I got a double meatball sub 'n soda down here for a Haywood, last name... Jablomi?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0530Amir_en.ogg)
- Vay Hek: "Huh. Oh. Hey. Which one of you festering tube rats is Haywood Jablomi? GRINEER! HAY-WOOD-JA-BLOW-ME? Nobody?! Wait..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0540VayHek_en.ogg)

- Vay Hek: "Tenno. When I find you I'm going to-" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DAmirNWRadioAmbient0560VayHek_en.ogg)

### Star Days/Quotes

[全セリフ](star-days.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Star_Days/Quotes)

**Idle**

- "We've got two families: biological and logical. Roky taught me that one. Star Day is for both those families... and anyone else besides." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0040Ticker_en.ogg)
- "'Star Day' is a Ticker tradition... begun for just one special person. Now... I give it to the world. If you want to know the story, you'll find it scattered across the Vallis. Today is too happy a day to tell it." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0050Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Two Ostrons are freed during a daring operation, marry, have a child. I'm sure you'll be pleased to know that young 'Tenno' Helnung is doing quite well and taking after their mother on the scaffolds." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0060Ticker_en.ogg)
- "It's trite, but it has to be said: be brave with that heart of yours, Stardust. At the end of the day, those we love are what matters. Don't leave things unsaid." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0070Ticker_en.ogg)
- "The only family that extraordinary creatures such as we are permitted is the family whom we choose - and a fine family that is. Here's to us, Stardust." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0080Ticker_en.ogg)
- "You raised yourself. You'll have questions, and nobody will be there to answer them. I want you to know: I'm here, Stardust. I won't always have the answer, but I will listen, and what I tell you will be true." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0090Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Spotted Biz and Little Duck boarding her rail tractor, bound for parts unknown. I guess they celebrate today in their own way. Lots of history those two. They'll work it out."' [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0100Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Nobody's as hard on Eudico as Eudico. She's made mistakes, and her demons are loud. I try to dazzle 'em with love. Millions are alive today because there's a light in there that people believe in. We don't often see things as they are, so much as we are." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0110Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Everybody's hurting, Stardust. Some of them hurt you. That's a given. Secret to a happy life? Find the ones worth hurting for. Happy Star Day." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0120Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Star Days celebrates life and love and the value of those closest to us. Heaven may have nicer weather, but you can't beat Hell for the company. Here's to you, Stardust." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0130Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Love isn't just for lovers, Stardust. It's for friends, family, your bestest Kavat. Love is an act of forgiveness, a tender look, a silent understanding. Today is about all of us and knowing that we have more binding us together than. Pushing. Us. Apart." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0140Ticker_en.ogg)
- "I was never much one for looks or physique. I longed for those who sang songs only I could hear. Falling into the eyes of someone like that, a girl feels like she's getting away with something." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0150Ticker_en.ogg)
- "I have loved, and I have lost. 'Their absence has gone through me like thread through a needle,' as someone once said. All that I do is stitched with their color." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0160Ticker_en.ogg)
- "Words and deeds are seeds, Stardust. They may seem like nothing in the moment, but they can fall from you and begin growing in others. Fortuna used to be barren, is what I'm saying. You've given us an orchard. Happy Star Day." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DStardayBrowseStore0170Ticker_en.ogg)

### Temporal Archimedea/Quotes

[全セリフ](temporal-archimedea.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/Temporal_Archimedea/Quotes)

**Kaya › Idle**

- Kaya: "Okay, get your loadout ready because once you're locked in, I can not pull you out without the whole run going to hell." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DTempArchPageIdle0310Kaya_en.ogg)
- Kaya: "Got a set of crisis events lined up for you. Make sure we get a gold star on each one." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DTempArchPageIdle0320Kaya_en.ogg)
- Kaya: "Velimir and Minerva are ready when you are. Stand by for temporal shenanigans!" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DTempArchPageIdle0330Kaya_en.ogg)
- During Temporal Archimedea

### The Descendia/Quotes

[全セリフ](the-descendia.md) / [Wiki](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/The_Descendia/Quotes)

**Infernum 7: Marie › Idle**

- Marie: "If we are going to stand about and collect dust, I would rather do it somewhere else, n'est-ce pas?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0770Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "I do not like lingering in the devil's mind. It makes me... itch." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0780Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "The longer we stay here, the more I start to feel like I have a tail. Can we go now, s'il vous plaît?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0790Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "Is there a purpose to your stalling, or have you fallen asleep?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0800Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "I do not think we can stand here forever. Ô Sol, tout sauf ça. Je ne veux pas être piégée dans l'esprit de ce singe pour tout l'éternité..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0810Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "Are we... lingering... for a reason? There must be a reason... n'est-ce pas?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0820Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "Are you looking for hidden prizes? Trésors de lutin, perhaps?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0830Marie_en.ogg)
- Marie: "I do not like the fighting, but... neither do I trust the silence. May we go?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DMarieFloorIdle0840Marie_en.ogg)

**Infernum 14: Lyon › Idle**

- Lyon: "And it is I who they would accuse of being part of the statuary." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1130Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "Does this idleness serve a purpose? Or has your mind simply wandered?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1140Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "Hm. I wonder if you have "transferred" elsewhere. Such as to fetch luncheon." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1150Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "We all have better tasks we could be seeing to." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1160Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "What, literally, are we waiting for?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1170Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "Are you afraid or... merely unprepared?" [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1180Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: [deep sigh] [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1190Lyon_en.ogg)
- Lyon: "This is becoming insufferable..." [音声](https://wiki.warframe.com/w/File:DLyonFloorIdle1200Lyon_en.ogg)
